triphalāmodakaḥ kuṣṭhādau
triphalā'ṣṭapalā kāryā bhallātaṃ ca catuṣpalam

hatalohaṃ trivṛccaiva gugguluśca śilājatu

citrakasya palārdhaṃsyād dviśāṇaṃ maricaṃ bhavet

bhagandaraplīhagulmāñjihvātālugalāmayān

prāgbhojanasya deyaṃ syādadhaḥ kāyasthite gade
bheṣajaṃ bhaktamadhye ca roge jaṭharasaṃsthite
bhojanasyopari grāhyamūrdhvajatrugadeṣu ca 94

gaṇḍamālāṃ jayatyugrāmapacīmarbudāni ca

kvāthaḥ khadirasārasya pathyākvāthoṣṇakaṃ jalam 100
māṣādimodakaḥ puṣṭau
nistuṣaṃ māṣacūrṇaṃ syāttathā godhūmasambhavam
nistuṣaṃ yavacūrṇaṃ ca śālitaṇḍulajaṃ tathā 101
sūkṣmaṃ ca pippalīcūrṇaṃ palikānyupakalpayet

ardhamātreṇa sarvebhyastataḥ khaṇḍaṃ samaṃ kṣipet

tataḥ pakvaṃ samuddhṛtya vṛttānkurvīta modakān
bhuktvā sāyaṃ palaikaṃ ca pibetkṣīraṃ caturguṇam 104
varjanīyau viśeṣeṇa kṣārāmlau dvau rasāvapi
kṛtvaivaṃ ramayennārīrbahvīrna kṣīyate naraḥ 105
iti śrīśārṅgadharasaṃhitāyāṃ madhyakhaṇḍe vaṭakakalpanānāma saptamo'dhyāyaḥ

atha avalehakalpanānāmāṣṭamo'dhyāyaḥ
avalehasya lakṣaṇanāmamātrānirdeśaḥ
kvāthādīnāṃ punaḥ pākādghanatvaṃ sā rasakriyā
so'valehaśca lehaḥ syāttanmātrā syātpalonmitā 1
anukte sitādīnāṃ parimāṇanirdeśaḥ
sitā caturguṇā kāryā cūrṇācca dviguṇo guḍaḥ
dra vaṃ caturguṇaṃ dadyāditi sarvatra niścayaḥ 2
avalehasya siddhilakṣaṇam
supakve tantumattvaṃ syādavaleho'psu majjati
kharatvaṃ pīḍite mudrā gandhavarṇarasodbhavaḥ 3
avalehasyānupānāni
dugdhamikṣuraso yūṣaḥ pañcamūlakaṣāyajaḥ
vāsākvātho yathāyogyamanupānaṃ praśasyate 4
kaṇṭakāryavalehaḥ kāsaśvāsādau
kaṇṭakārītulāṃ nīradro ṇe paktvā kaṣāyakam
pādaśeṣaṃ gṛhītvā ca tasmiṃścūrṇāni dāpayet 5
pṛthakpalāni caitāni guḍūcīcavyacitrakāḥ
mustaṃ karkaṭaśṛṅgī ca tr! yūṣaṇaṃ dhanvayāsakaḥ 6
bhārṅgī rāsnā śaṭī caiva śarkarā palaviṃśatiḥ
pratyekaṃ ca palānyaṣṭau pradadyād ghṛtatailayoḥ 7
paktvā lehatvamānīya śīte madhupalāṣṭakam
catuṣpalaṃ tugākṣīryāḥ pippalīnāṃ catuṣpalam 8
kṣiptvā nidadhyātsudṛḍhe mṛṇmaye bhājane śubhe
leho'ya hanti hikkā'ttiśvāsakāsānaśeṣataḥ 9
cyavanaprāśaḥ kṣayakṣīṇādau
pāṭalā'raṇikāśmaryabilvāralukagokṣurāḥ
parṇyau bṛhatyau pippalyaḥ śṛṅgī drā kṣā'mṛtā'bhayā 10
balā bhūmyāmalī vāsāṛddhirjīvantikā śaṭī
jīvakarṣabhakau mustaṃ pauṣkaraṃ kākanāsikā 11
mudgaparṇīṃ māṣaparṇī vidārī ca punarnavā
kākolyau kamalaṃ mede sūkṣmailāgarucandanam 12
ekaikaṃ palasammānaṃ sthūlacūrṇitamauṣadham
ekīkṛtya bṛhatpātre pañcāmalaśatāni ca 13
paceddro ṇajalekṣiptvāgrāhyamaṣṭāṃśaśeṣitam
tatastutānyāmalāniniṣkulīkṛtyavāsasā 14
dṛḍhahastena sampīḍyakṣiptvā tatra tatoghṛtam
palasaptamitaṃ tāni kiñcid bhṛṣṭvā'lpavahninā 15
tatastatra kṣipetkvāthaṃ khaṇḍaṃ cārdhatulonmitam
lehavatsādhayitvā ca cūrṇānīmāni dāpayet 16
pippalī dvipalā deyā tugākṣīrī catuṣpalā
pratyekaṃ ca triśāṇaṃ syāttvagelāpatrakeśarāḥ 17
tatastvekīkṛte tasminkṣipetkṣaudraṃ ca ṣaṭpalam
ityetaccyavanaproktaṃ cyavanaprāśasaṃjñitam 18
lehaṃ vahnibalaṃ dṛṣṭvā khādetkṣīṇo rasāyanam
bālavṛddhakṣatakṣīṇā nārīkṣīṇāśca śoṣiṇaḥ 19
hṛdro giṇaḥ svarakṣīṇā ye narāsteṣu yujyate
kāsaṃ śvāsaṃ pipāsāṃ ca vātāsramuraso graham 20
vātapittaṃ śukradoṣaṃ mūtradoṣaṃ ca nāśayet
medhāṃ smṛtiṃ strīṣu harṣaṃ kāntiṃ varṇaprasannatām
asya prayogādāpnoti naro jīrṇavivarjitaḥ 21
kūṣmāṇḍāvaleho raktapittādau
niṣkulīkṛtya kūṣmāṇḍakhaṇḍānpalaśataṃ pacet
nikṣipya dvitulaṃ nīramardhaśiṣṭaṃ ca gṛhyate 22
tāni kūṣmāṇḍakhaṇḍāni pīḍayed dṛḍhavāsasā
ātape śoṣayetkiñcicchūlāgrairbahuśovyadhet 23
kṣiptvā tāmrakaṭāhe ca dadyādaṣṭapalaṃ ghṛtam
tena kiñcidbharjayitvā pūrvoktaṃ tajjalaṃ kṣipet 24
khaṇḍaṃ palaśataṃ dattvā sarvamekatra pācayet
supakve pippalī śuṇṭhī jīrakaṃ dvipalaṃ pṛthak 25
pṛthakpalārdhaṃ dhānyākaṃ patrailāmaricaṃtvacam
cūrṇīkṛtya kṣipettatra ghṛtārdhaṃ kṣaudra māvahet 26
khādedagnibalaṃ dṛṣṭvā raktapittī kṣayī jvarī
śoṣatṛṣṇābhramacchardiśvāsakāsakṣatāturaḥ 27
kūṣmāṇḍakāvaleho'ya bālavṛddheṣu yujyate
uraḥsandhānakṛd vṛṣyo bṛṃhaṇo balakṛnmataḥ 28
khaṇḍaśūraṇāvaleho'śoroge
yuktyā kūṣmāṇḍakhaṇḍasya śūraṇaṃ vipacetsudhīḥ
arśasāṃ mūḍhavātānāṃ mandāgnīnāṃ ca yujyate 29
agastyaharītakyavalehaḥ kṣayādau
harītakīśataṃ bhadraṃ yavānāmāḍhakaṃ tathā 30
palāni daśamūlasya viṃśatiṃ ca niyojayet
citrakaḥ pippalīmūlamapāmārgaḥ śaṭī tathā 31
kapikacchūḥ śaṃkhapuṣpī bhārṅgī ca gajapippalī
balā puṣkaramūlaṃ ca pṛthagdvipalamātrayā 32
pacetpañcāḍhake nīre yavaiḥ svinnaiḥ śṛtaṃ nayet
taccābhayāśataṃdadyātkvāthe tasminvicakṣaṇaḥ 33
sarpistailāṣṭapalakaṃ kṣipedguḍatulāṃ tathā
paktvā lehatvamānīya siddhe śīte pṛthakpṛthak 34
kṣaudraṃ ca pippalīcūrṇaṃ dadyātkuḍavamātrayā
harītakīdvayaṃ khādettena lehena nityaśaḥ 35
kṣayaṃ kāsaṃ jvaraṃ śvāsaṃ hikkā'śo'rucipīnasān
grahaṇīṃ nāśayedeṣa valīpalitanāśanaḥ 36
balavarṇakaraḥ puṃsāmavaleho rasāyanam
vihito'gastyamuninā sarvarogapraṇāśanaḥ 37
kuṭajāvaleho'śa ādirogeṣu
kuṭajatvaktulāṃ dro ṇe jalasya vipacetsudhīḥ
kaṣāyaṃ pādaśeṣaṃ ca gṛhṇīyādvastragālitam 38
triṃśatpalaṃguḍasyātra dattvā ca vipacetpunaḥ
sāndra tvamāgataṃ jñātvā cūrṇānīmāni dāpayet 39
rasāñjanaṃ mocarasaṃ trikaṭu triphalāṃ tathā
lajjāluṃ citrakaṃ pāṭhāṃ bilvamindra yavaṃ tvacam 40
bhallātakaṃ prativiṣāṃ viḍaṅgāni ca bālakam
pratyekaṃ palasaṃmānaṃ ghṛtasya kuḍavaṃ tathā 41
siddhaśīte tato dadyānmadhunaḥ kuḍavaṃ tathā
jayedeṣo'valehastu sarvāṇyarśāṃsi vegataḥ 42
durnāmaprabhavānrogānatīsāramarocakam
grahaṇīṃ pāṇḍurogaṃ ca raktapittaṃ ca kāmalām 43
amlapittaṃ tathā śothaṃ kārśyaṃ caiva pravāhikām
anupāne prayoktavyamājaṃ takraṃ payo dadhi
ghṛtaṃ jalaṃ vā jīrṇe ca pathyabhojī bhavennaraḥ 44
kuṭajāṣṭakādyavaleho'tīsārādau
kuṭajatvaktulāmādrā raṃ! dro ṇanīre vipācayet
pādaśeṣaṃ śṛtaṃ nītvā cūrṇānyetāni dāpayet 45
lajjālurdhātakī bilvaṃ pāṭhā mocarasastathā
mustaṃ prativiṣā caiva pratyekaṃ syātpalaṃ palam 46
tatastu vipaced bhūyo yāvaddarvīpralepanam
jalena chāgadugdhena pīto maṇḍena vā jayet 47
sarvātisārānghorāṃstu nānāvarṇānsavedanān
asṛgdaraṃ samastaṃ ca sarvārśāṃsi pravāhikām 48
iti śrīśārṅgadharasaṃhitāyāṃ madhyakhaṇḍe'valehakalpanānāmāṣṭamo'dhyāyaḥ
snehakalpanā
atha snehakalpanānāma navamo'dhyāyaḥ
ghṛtatailakalpanā
kalkāccaturguṇīkṛtya ghṛtaṃ vā tailameva vā
caturguṇe dra ve sādhyaṃ tasya mātrā palonmitā 1
snehasādhanārthaṃ kvāthaparibhāṣā
nikṣipya kvāthayettoyaṃ kvāthyadra vyāccaturguṇam
pādaśiṣṭaṃ gṛhītvā ca snehaṃ tenaiva sādhayet 2
mṛdudra vyādau kvathane jalapramāṇam
caturguṇaṃ mṛdudra vye kaṭhine'ṣṭaguṇaṃ jalam
tathā ca madhyame dra vye dadyādaṣṭaguṇaṃ payaḥ
atyantakaṭhine dra vye nīraṃ ṣoḍaśakaṃ matam 3
matāntare parimāṇaviśeṣaḥ
karṣāditaḥ palaṃ yāvatkṣipetṣoḍaśakaṃ jalam 4
tadūrdhvaṃ kuḍavaṃ yāvadbhavedaṣṭaguṇaṃ payaḥ
prasthāditaḥ kṣipennīraṃ khārīṃ yāvaccaturguṇam 5
ambvādibhiryatra snehasādhanaṃ tatra kalkapramāṇam
ambukvātharasairyatra pṛthaksnehasya sādhanam
kalkasyāṃśaṃ tatra dadyāccaturthaṃ ṣaṣṭhamaṣṭamam 6
dugdhādipākaparibhāṣā
dugdhe dadhni rase takre kalko deyo'ṣṭamāṃśakaḥ
kalkasya samyakpākārthaṃ toyamatra caturguṇam 7
snehe pañcādhikadra vavyavasthā
dra vāṇi yatra sneheṣu pañcādīni bhavanti hi
tatra snehasamānyāhuryathāpūrvaṃ caturguṇam 8
kevaladra vyasya snehapākārthaṃ mānam
dra vyeṇa kevalenaiva snehapāko bhavedyadi
tatrāmbupiṣṭaḥ kalkaḥ syājjalaṃ cātra caturguṇam 9
kevalakvāthasya snehapākārthaṃ mānam
kvāthena kevalenaiva pāko yatreritaḥ kvacit
kvāthyadra vyasya kalko'pi tatra snehe prayujyate 10
kalkahīnasnehapākavidhiḥ
kalkahīnastu yaḥ snehaḥ sa sādhyaḥ kevale dra ve 11
puṣpakalkasnehapākavidhiḥ
puṣpakalkastu yaḥ snehastatra toyaṃ caturguṇam
snehe snehāṣṭamāṃśaśca puṣpakalkaḥ prayujyate 12
snehasiddhalakṣaṇam
varttivatsnehakalkaḥ syādyadāṅgulyāvimarditaḥ
śabdahīnogninikṣiptaḥsnehaḥsiddhobhavettadā 13
ghṛtatailapākayoḥ siddhau svarūpanirdeśaḥ
yadā phenodgamastaile phenaśāntiśca sarpiṣi
gandhavarṇarasotpattiḥ snehaḥ siddhastadā bhavet 14
snehapākasya bhedāstallakṣaṇāni ca
snehapākastridhā prokto mṛdurmadhyaḥ kharastathā
īṣatsarasakalkastu snehapāko mṛdurbhavet 15
madhyapākasya siddhiśca kalke nīrasakomale
īṣatkaṭhinakalkaśca snehapāko bhavetkharaḥ
tadūrdhvaṃ dagdhapākaḥ syāddāhakṛnniṣprayojanaḥ 16
āmapākasnehaguṇāḥ
āmapākaśca nirvīryo vahnimāndyakaro guruḥ 17
mṛdvādipākasnehānāṃ niyamāḥ
nasyārthaṃ syānmṛduḥ pāko madhyamaḥ sarvakarmasu
abhyaṅgārthaṃ kharaḥ prokto yuñjyādevaṃ yathocitam 18
ghṛtādisādhane kālanirdeśaḥ
ghṛtatailaguḍādīṃśca sādhayennaikavāsare
prakurvantyuṣitā hyete viśeṣād guṇasañcayam 19
atha ghṛtakalpanā
kṣīraṣaṭpalaghṛtaṃ viṣamajvarādau
pippalīpippalīmūlacavyacitrakanāgaraiḥ
sasaindhavaiśca palikairghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet
kṣīraṃ caturguṇaṃ dattvā tad ghṛtaṃ plīhanāśanam
viṣamajvaramandāgniharaṃ rucikaraṃ param 20
cāṅgerīghṛtamatīsāragrahaṇyoḥ
pippalī pippalīmūlaṃ citrako hastipippalī
śvadaṃṣṭrā nāgaraṃ dhānyaṃ pāṭhā bilvaṃ yavānikā 21
dra vyaiśca palikairetaiścatuḥ ṣaṣṭipalaṃ ghṛtam
ghṛtāccaturguṇaṃ dadyāccāṅgerīsvarasaṃ budhaḥ 22
tathā caturguṇaṃ dattvā dadhi sarpirvipācayet
śanaiḥ śanairvipaktavyaṃ cāṅgerīghṛtamuttamam 23
tad ghṛtaṃ kaphavātaghnaṃ grahaṇyarśovikāranut
hantyānāhaṃ gudabhraṃśaṃ mūtrakṛcchraṃ pravāhikām 24
masūraghṛtamatīsārādau
masūrāṇāṃ palaśataṃ nīradro ṇe vipācayet
pādaśeṣaṃ śṛtaṃ nītvā dattvā bilvapalāṣṭakam 25
ghṛtaprasthaṃ pacettena sarvātīsāranāśanam
grahaṇīṃ bhinnaviṭkaṃ ca nāśayecca pravāhikām 26
kāmadevaghṛtaṃ raktapittādau
aśvagandhā tulaikā syāttadardho gokṣuraḥ smṛtaḥ
balā'mṛtā śāliparṇī vidārī ca śatāvarī 27
aśvatthasya ca śuṅgāni padmabījaṃ punarnavā
kāśmaryāśca phalaṃ caiva māṣabījaṃ tathaiva ca 28
pṛthagdaśapalān bhāgām̐ ścatudro rṇe!'mbhasi pacet
dro ṇaśeṣe rase tasmin paceccaiva ghṛtāḍhakam 29
mṛdvīkā padmakaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ pippalī raktacandanam
patrakaṃ nāgapuṣpaṃ ca ātmaguptāphalaṃ tathā 30
nīlotpalaṃ sārive dve jīvanīyo gaṇastathā
pṛthak karṣasamā bhāgāḥ śarkarāyāḥ paladvayam 31
rasaśca pauṇḍrakekṣūṇāmāḍhakaikaṃ samāharet
caturguṇena payasā ghṛtāḍhakaṃ vipācayet 32
ghṛtametannihantyāśu raktapittamuraḥkṣatam
halīmakaṃ pāṇḍurogaṃ varṇabhedaṃ svarakṣayam 33
mūtrakṛcchramurodāhaṃ pārśvaśūlaṃ ca nāśayet
etat sarpiḥ prayoktavyaṃ bahvantaḥ puravāsinām 34
strīṇāṃ caivāprajātānāṃ durbalānāṃ ca dehinām
śreṣṭhaṃ balakaraṃ varṇyaṃ hṛdyaṃ puṣṭirasāyanam 35
ojastejaskaraṃ hṛdyamāyuṣyaṃ prāṇavarddhanam
saṃvarddhayati śukrasya puruṣaṃ durbalendri yam 36
sarvarogavinirmuktaḥ payaḥsikto yathā drumaḥ
kāmadeva iti khyātaṃ sarpiruktaṃ mahāguṇam 37
pānīyakalyāṇakaṃ ghṛtamapasmārādau
triphalā dve niśe kauntī sārive dve priyaṅgukā
śāliparṇī pṛṣṭhiparṇī devadārvyelavālukam 38
nataṃ viśālā dantī ca dāḍimaṃ nāgakeśaram
nīlotpalailā mañjiṣṭhā viḍaṅgaṃ kuṣṭhapadmakam 39
jātipuṣpaṃ candanaṃ ca tālīsaṃ bṛhatī tathā
etaiḥ karṣasamaiḥ kalkairjalaṃ dattvā caturguṇam 40
ghṛtaprasthaṃ paceddhīmānapasmāre jvare kṣaye
unmāde vātarakte ca kāse mandānale tathā 41
pratiśyāye kaṭīśūle tṛtīyaka caturthake
mūtrakṛcchre visarpe ca kaṇḍūpāṇḍvāmaye tathā 42
viṣadvaye prameheṣu sarvathaivopayujyate
vandhyānāṃ putradaṃ bhūtayakṣarakṣoharaṃ smṛtam 43
amṛtāghṛtaṃ kuṣṭhādau
amṛtākvāthakalkābhyāṃ sakṣīraṃ vipaced ghṛtam
vātaraktaṃ jayatyāśu kuṣṭhaṃ jayati dustaram 44
mahātiktakaghṛtaṃ vātaraktakuṣṭhādau
saptacchadaḥ prativiṣā śamyākaḥ kaṭurohiṇī
pāṭhā mustamuśīraṃ ca triphalā parpaṭastathā 45
paṭolanimbamañjiṣṭhāḥ pippalī padmakaṃ śaṭī
candanaṃ dhanvayāsaśca viśāle dve niśe tathā 46
guḍūcī sārive dve ca mūrvā vāsā śatāvarī
trāyantīndra yavā yaṣṭī bhūnimbaścākṣabhāgikāḥ 47
ghṛtaṃ caturguṇaṃ dadyāt ghṛtādāmalakīrasaḥ
dviguṇaḥ sarpiṣaścātra jalamaṣṭaguṇaṃ bhavet 48
tatsiddhaṃ pāyayetsarpirvātarakteṣu sarvathā
kuṣṭhāni raktapittaṃ ca raktārśāṃsi ca pāṇḍutām 49
hṛdro gagulmavīsarpapradaraṃ gaṇḍamālikām
kṣudra rogāñjvarāṃścaiva mahātiktamidaṃ jayet 50
kāsīsādyaghṛtaṃ kuṣṭhe dadrū pāmāśiraḥ sphoṭādau
kāsīsaṃ dve niśe mustaṃ haritālaṃ manaḥ śilām
kampillakaṃ gandhakaṃ ca viḍaṅgaṃ gugguluṃ tathā 51
sikthakaṃ maricaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ tutthakaṃ gaurasarṣapān
rasāñjanaṃ ca sindūraṃ śrīvāsaṃ raktacandanam 52
irimedaṃ nimbapatraṃ karañjaṃ sārivāṃ vacām
mañjiṣṭhāṃ madhukaṃ māṃsīṃ śirīṣaṃ lodhrapadmakam 53
harītakīṃ prapunnāṭaṃ cūrṇayetkārṣikānpṛthak
tatastaccūrṇamāloḍya triṃśatpalamite ghṛte 54
sthāpayettāmrapātre ca gharme saptadināni vai
asyābhyaṅgena kuṣṭhāni dadrupāmāvicarcikāḥ 55
śūkadoṣā visarpāśca visphoṭā vātaraktajāḥ
śiraḥ sphoṭopadaṃśāśca nāḍīduṣṭavraṇāni ca 56
śotho bhagandaraścaiva lūtāḥ śāmyanti dehinām
śodhanaṃ ropaṇaṃ caiva savarṇakaraṇaṃ ghṛtam 57
jātyādighṛtaṃ vraṇe
jātīnimbapaṭolāśca dve niśe kaṭurohiṇī
mañjiṣṭhā madhukaṃ sikthaṃ karañjośīrasārivāḥ 58
tutthaṃ ca vipacetsamyakkalkairebhirghṛtaṃ budhaḥ
asya lepātprarohanti sūkṣmanāḍīvraṇā api 59
marmāśritāḥ kledinaśca gambhīrāḥ sarujo vraṇāḥ 60
bindughṛtaṃ jalodaravirecanādau
citrakaḥ śaṅkhinī pathyā kaṃpillāstrivṛtāyugam
vṛddhadāruśca śamyāko dantī daṃtīphalaṃ tathā 61
kośātakī devadālī nīlinī girikarṇikā
sātalā pippalīmūlaṃ viḍaṅgaṃ kuṭakī tathā 62
hemakṣīrī ca vipacetkalkairebhiḥ picūnmitaiḥ
ghṛtaprasthaṃ snuhīkṣīre ṣaṭpale tu paladvaye 63
arkakṣīrasya matimāṃstatsiddhaṃ gulmakuṣṭhahṛt
hanti śūlamudāvarttaṃ śothādhmānaṃ bhagandaram 64
śamayatyudarāṇyaṣṭau nipātaṃ bindusaṅkhyayā
godugdhenoṣṭradugdhena kaulatthena śṛtena vā 65
uṣṇodakena vā pītvā binduvegairvirecayet
etadvindughṛtaṃ nāma nābhilepādvi recayet 66
triphalādyaṃ ghṛtaṃ netraroge timirādau
triphalāyā rasaprasthaṃ prasthaṃ vāsārasodbhavam
bhṛṅgarājarasaprasthaṃ prasthamājaṃ payaḥ smṛtam
dattvā tatra ghṛtaprasthaṃ kalkaiḥ karṣamitaiḥ pṛthak 67
triphalā pippalī drā kṣā candanaṃ saindhavaṃ balā
kākolī kṣīrakākolī medā maricanāgaram 68
śarkarā puṇḍarīkaṃ ca kamalaṃ ca punarnavā
niśāyugmaṃ ca madhukaṃ sarvai rebhirvipācayet 69
naktāndhyaṃ nakulāndhyaṃ ca kaṇḍūṃ pillaṃ tathaiva ca
netrasrāvaṃ ca paṭalaṃ timiraṃ kācakaṃ jayet 70
anye'pi praśamaṃ yānti netrarogāḥ sudāruṇāḥ
traiphalaṃ ghṛtametaddhi pāne nasyādiṣūcitam 71
gaurādyaṃ ghṛtaṃ vraṇe
dve haridre sthirā mūrvā sārivā candanadvayam
madhuparṇī ca madhukapadmakeśarapadmakaiḥ 72
utpalośīramedābhistriphalāpañcavalkalaiḥ
kalkaiḥ karṣamitairetairghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet 73
visarpalūtāvisphoṭaviṣakīṭavraṇāpaham
gaurādyamiti vikhyātaṃ sarpirviṣaharaṃ param 74
mayūraghṛtaṃ śirorogādau
balāmadhukarāsnābhirdaśamūlaphalatrikaiḥ
pṛthagdvipalikairebhidro rṇa!nīreṇa pācayet 75
mayūraṃ pakṣapittāntrayakṛtpādāsyavarjitam
pādaśeṣaṃ śṛtaṃ nītvā kṣīraṃ dattvā ca tatsamam 76
ghṛtaprasthaṃ pacetsamyagjīvanīyaiḥ picūnmitaiḥ
tatsiddhaṃ śirasaḥ pīḍāṃ manyāpṛṣṭhagrahaṃ tathā 77
arditaṃ karṇanāsā'kṣijihvāgalarujo jayet
pāne nasye tathā'bhyaṅge karṇapūreṣu yujyate 78
hemantakālaśiśiravasanteṣu ca śasyate 79
phalaghṛtaṃ vandhyādoṣe
triphalā madhukaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ dve niśe kaṭurohiṇī
viḍaṅgaṃ pippalī mustā viśālā kaṭphalaṃ vacā 80
dve mede dve ca kākolyau sārive dve priyaṅgukā
śatapuṣpā hiṅgu rāsnā candanaṃ raktacandanam 81
jātī puṣpaṃ tugākṣīrī kamalaṃ śarkarā tathā
ajamodā ca dantī ca kalkairetaiśca kārṣikaiḥ 82
jīvadvatsaikavarṇāyā ghṛtaprasthaṃ ca goḥ kṣipet
caturguṇena payasā pacedāraṇyagomayaiḥ 83
sutithau puṣyanakṣatre mṛdbhāṇḍe tāmraje tathā
tataḥ pibecchubhadine nārī vā puruṣo'thavā 84
etatsarpirnaraḥ pītvā strīṣu nityaṃ vṛṣāyate
putrānutpādayeddhīmānvandhyā'pi labhate sutam 85
anāyuṣaṃ yā janayedyā ca sūtā punaḥ sthitā
putraṃ prāpnoti sā nārī buddhimantaṃ śatāyuṣam 86
etatphalaghṛtaṃ nāma bharadvājena bhāṣitam
anuktaṃ lakṣmaṇāmūlaṃ kṣipettatra cikitsakaḥ 87
laghuphalaghṛtaṃ yoniroge
triphalāṃ dve sahacare guḍūcīṃ sapunarnavām
śukanāsāṃ haridre dve rāsnāṃ medāṃ śatāvarīm 88
kalkīkṛtya ghṛtaprasthaṃ pacetkṣīre caturguṇe
tatsiddhaṃpāyayennārīṃ yoniśūlanipīḍitām 89
pīḍitā calitā yā ca niḥsṛtā vivṛtā ca yā
pittayoniśca vibhrāntā ṣaṇḍhayoniśca yā smṛtā 90
prapadyante hitāḥ sthānaṃ garbhaṃ gṛhṇanti cāsakṛt
etatphalaghṛtaṃ nāma yonidoṣaharaṃ param 91
pañcatittakaṃ ghṛtaṃ viṣamajvarādau
vṛṣanimbāmṛtāvyāghrīpaṭolānāṃ śṛtena ca
kalkena pakvaṃ sarpistu nihanyādviṣamajvarān
pāṇḍuṃ kuṣṭhaṃ visarpaṃ ca kṛmīnarśāṃsi nāśayet 92
atha taila kalpanā
lākṣāditailaṃ viṣamajvarādau
lākṣāḍhakaṃ kvāthayitvā jalasya caturāḍhakaiḥ
caturthāṃśaṃ śṛtaṃ nītvā tailaprasthe vinikṣipet 93
mastvāḍhakaṃ ca godadhnastatraiva viniyojayet
śatapuṣpāmaśvagandhāṃ haridrā ṃ! devadāru ca 94
kuṭakīṃ reṇukāṃ mūrvāṃ kuṣṭhaṃ ca madhuyaṣṭikām
candanaṃ mustakaṃ rāsnāṃ pṛthakkarṣapramāṇataḥ 95
cūrṇayettatra nikṣipya sādhayenmṛduvahninā
asyābhyaṅgātpraśāmyanti sarve'pi viṣamajvarāḥ 96
kāsaśvāsapratiśyāyatrikapṛṣṭhagrahāstathā
vātaṃ pittamapasmāramunmādaṃ yakṣarākṣasān 97
kaṇḍūśūlaṃ ca daurgandhyaṃ gātrāṇāṃ sphuraṇaṃ jayet
puṣṭagarbhā bhavedasya garbhiṇyabhyaṅgato bhṛśam 98
aṅgāratailaṃ sarvajvareṣu
mūrvā lākṣā haridre dve mañjiṣṭhā sendra vāruṇī
bṛhatī saindhavaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ rāsnā māṃsī śatāvarī 99
āranālāḍhake tatra tailaprasthaṃ vipācayet
tailamaṅgārakaṃ nāma sarvajvaravimokṣaṇam 100
nārāyaṇatailaṃ vātarogādau
aśvagandhāṃ balāṃ bilvaṃ pāṭalāṃ bṛhatīdvayam
śvadaṃṣṭrā'tibalānimbaṃ syonākaṃ ca punarnavām 101
prasāriṇīmagnimanthaṃ kuryāddaśapalaṃ pṛthak
catudro rṇe! jale paktvā pādaśeṣaṃ śṛtaṃ nayet 102
tailāḍhakena saṃyojya śatāvaryā rasāḍhakam
kṣipettatra ca gokṣīraṃ tailāttasmāccaturguṇam 103
śanairvipācayedebhiḥ kalkairdvipalikaiḥ pṛthak
kuṣṭhailācandanaṃ mūrvā vacā māṃsī sasaindhavaiḥ 104
aśvagandhāvacārāsnāśatapuṣpendra dārubhiḥ
parṇīcatuṣṭayenaiva tagareṇa ca sādhayed 105
tattailaṃ nāvane'bhyaṅge pāne bastau na yojayet
pakṣāghātaṃ hanustambhaṃ manyāstambhaṃ galagraham 106
khallatvaṃ badhiratvaṃ ca gatibhaṅgaṃ kaṭigraham
gātraśoṣendri yadhvaṃsāvasṛkśukrajvarakṣayān 107
antravṛddhiṃ kuraṇḍaṃ ca dantarogaṃ śirograham
pārśvaśūlaṃ ca pāṅgulyaṃ buddhihāniṃ ca gṛdhrasīm 108
anyāṃśca viṣamānvātāñjayetsarvāṅgasaṃśrayān
asya prabhāvādvandhyā'pi nārī putraṃ prasūyate 109
martyo gajo vā turagastailādasmātsukhī bhavet
yathā nārāyaṇo devo duṣṭadaityavināśanaḥ 110
tathaiva vātarogāṇāṃ nāśanaṃ tailamuttamam 111
vāruṇītailaṃ kamparoge
vāruṇyā auttaraṃ mūlaṃ kuṭṭitaṃ tu palatrayam
paladvādaśakaṃ tailaṃ kṣaṇaṃ vahnau vipācitam 112
niṣkatrayaṃ bhaktayuktaṃ sevetāsmādvinaśyati
hastakampaḥ śiraḥkampaḥ kampo manyāśirābhavaḥ 113
balādyaṃ tailaṃ vātavyādhau
balāmūlakaṣāyeṇa daśamūlaśṛtena ca
kulatthayavakolānāṃ kvāthena payasā tathā 114
aṣṭāṣṭabhāgayuktena bhāgamekaṃ ca tailakam
gaṇena jīvanīyena śatāvaryendra dāruṇā 115
mañjiṣṭhākuṣṭhaśaileyatagarāgarusaindhavaiḥ
vacāpunarnavāmāṃsīsārivādvayapatrakaiḥ 116
śatapuṣpā'śvagaṃdhābhyāmelayā ca vipācayet
garbhārthinīnāṃ nārīṇāṃ narāṇāṃkṣīṇaretasām 117
vyāyāmakṣīṇagātrāṇāṃ sūtikānāṃ ca yujyate
rājayogyamidaṃ tailaṃ sukhināṃ ca viśeṣataḥ
balātailamiti khyātaṃ sarvavātāmayāpaham 118
prasāriṇītailaṃ vātakapharogādau
prasāriṇīpalaśataṃ jaladro ṇe vipācayet
pādaśiṣṭaḥ śṛto grāhyastailaṃ dadhi ca tatsamam 119
kāñjikaṃ ca samaṃ tailātkṣīraṃ tailāccaturguṇam
tailāttathā'ṣṭamāṃśena sarvakalkāni yojayet 120
madhukaṃ pippalīmūlaṃ citrakaḥ saindhavaṃ vacā
prasāriṇī devadāru rāsnā ca gajapippalī 121
bhallātaḥ śatapuṣpā ca māṃsī caibhirvipācayet
etattailaṃ varaṃ pakvaṃ vātaśleṣmāmayāñjayet 122
kaubjaṃ paṅgutvakhañjatve gṛdhrasīmarditaṃ tathā
hanupṛṣṭhaśirogrīvākaṭistambhānvināśayet 123
anyāṃśca viṣamānvātānsarvānāśu vyapohati 124
māṣāditailaṃ vātavikārādau
māṣā yavātasī kṣudrā markaṭī ca kuraṇṭakaḥ
gokaṇṭaṣṭuṇṭukaścaiṣāṃ kuryātsaptapalaṃ pṛthak 125
caturguṇāmbunā paktvā pādaśeṣaṃ śṛtaṃ nayet
karpāsāsthīni badaraṃ śaṇabījaṃ kulatthakam 126
pṛthakcaturdaśapalaṃ caturguṇajale pacet
caturthāṃśāvaśiṣṭaṃ ca gṛhṇīyātkvāthamuttamam 127
prasthaikaṃ chāgamāṃsasya catuḥṣaṣṭipale jale
nikṣipya pācayeddhīmānpādaśeṣaṃ śṛtaṃ nayet 128
tailaprasthe tataḥ sarvānkvāthānetānvinikṣipet
kalkairebhiśca vipacedamṛtākuṣṭhanāgaraiḥ 129
rāsnāpunarnavairaṇḍaiḥ pippalyā śatapuṣpayā
balāprasāriṇībhyāṃ ca māṃsyā kaṭukayā tathā 130
pṛthagarddhapalairetaiḥ sādhayenmṛduvahninā
hanyāttailamidaṃ śīghraṃ grīvāstambhāpabāhukau 131
ardhāṅgaśoṣamākṣepamūrustambhāpatānakau
śākhākampaṃ śiraḥ kampaṃ viśvācīmarditaṃ tathā
māṣādikamidaṃ tailaṃ sarvavātavikāranut 132
śatāvarītailaṃ vātādau
śatāvarī balāyugmaṃ parṇyau gandharvahastakaḥ
aśvagandhā śvadaṃṣṭraśca bilvaḥ kāśaḥ kuraṇṭakaḥ 133
eṣāṃ sārddhapalānbhāgānkalpayecca vipācayet
caturguṇena nīreṇa pādaśeṣaṃ śṛtaṃ nayet 134
niyojya tailaprasthe ca kṣīraprasthaṃ vinikṣipet
śatāvarīrasaprasthaṃ jalaprasthaṃ ca yojayet 135
śatāvarīdevadārumāṃsītagaracandanam
śatapuṣpā balā kuṣṭhamelā śaileyamutpalam 136
ṛddhirmedā ca madhukaṃ kākolī jīvakastathā
eṣāṃ karṣasamaiḥ kalkaistailaṃ gomayavahninā 137
pacettenaiva tailena strīṣu nityaṃ vṛṣāyate
nārī ca labhate putraṃ yoniśūlaṃ ca naśyati 138
aṅgaśūlaṃ śiraḥ śūlaṃ kāmalāṃ pāṇḍutāṃ garam
gṛdhrasīplīhaśoṣāṃśca mehāndaṇḍāpatānakam 139
sadāhaṃ vātaraktaṃ ca vātapittagadārditam
asṛgdaraṃ tathādhmānaṃ raktapittaṃ ca naśyati 140
śatāvarītailamidaṃ kṛṣṇātreyeṇa bhāṣitam
oṃ nārāyaṇyai svāhā uttarābhimukho bhūtvā khanetkhadiraśaṅkunā 141
oṃ sarvavyādhināśinyai svāhā ityutpāṭanamantraḥ
oṃ kumārajīvinyai svāhā iti pānamantraḥ 142
kāsīsādyaṃ tailamarśa ādau
kāsīsaṃ lāṅgalī kuṣṭhaṃ śuṇṭhī kṛṣṇā ca saindhavam
manaḥ śilāśvamāraśca viḍaṅgaṃ citrako vṛṣaḥ 143
dantī kośātakībījaṃ hemāhvā haritālakam
kalkaiḥ karṣamitairetaistailaprasthaṃ vipācayet 144
snuhyarkapayasī dadyātpṛthagdvipalasaṃmite
caturguṇaṃ gavāṃ mūtraṃ dattvā samyakprasādhayet 145
kathitaṃ kharanādena tailamarśovināśanam
kṣāravatpātayatyetadarśāṃsyabhyaṅgato bhṛśam
valīrna dūṣayatyetatkṣārakarmakaraṃ smṛtam 146
piṇḍatailaṃ vātarakte
mañjiṣṭhāsārivāsarjayaṣṭīsikthaiḥ palonmitaiḥ
piṇḍākhyaṃ sādhayettailamabhyaṅgāt vātaraktanut 147
arkatailaṃ kuṣṭharogādau
arkapatrarase pakvaṃ haridrā kalkasaṃyutam
nāśayetsārṣapaṃ tailaṃ pāmāṃ kacchūṃ vicarcikām 148
maricāditailaṃ kuṣṭhavraṇādau
maricaṃ haritālaṃ ca trivṛtaṃ raktacandanam
mustaṃ manaḥśilā māṃsī dve niśe devadāru ca 149
viśālā karavīraṃ ca kuṣṭhamarkapayastathā
tathaiva gomayarasaṃ kuryātkarṣamitānpṛthak 150
viṣaṃ cārddhapalaṃ deyaṃ prasthaṃ ca kaṭutailakam
gomūtraṃ dviguṇaṃ dadyājjalaṃ ca dviguṇaṃ bhavet 151
maricādyamidaṃ tailaṃ sidhmakuṣṭhaharaṃ param
jayetkuṣṭhāni sarvāṇi puṇḍarīkaṃ vicarcikām
pāmāṃ śvitrāṇi raktaṃ ca kaṇḍūṃ kacchūṃ praṇāśayet 152
triphalāditailamarūṃṣikāyām
triphalā'riṣṭabhūnimbaṃ dve niśe raktacandanam
etaiḥ siddhamarūṃṣīṇāṃ tailamabhyajjane hitam 153
nimbabījatailaṃ palite
bhāvayennimbabījāni bhṛṅgarājarasena hi
tathā'sanasya toyena tattailaṃ hanti nasyataḥ 154
akālapalitaṃ sadyaḥ puṃsāṃ dugdhānnabhojinām 155
yaṣṭīmadhukatailaṃ khalite
yaṣṭīmadhukakṣārābhyāṃ navadhātrīphalaiḥ śṛtam
tailaṃ nasyakṛtaṃ kuryātkeśāñśmaśrūṇi saṅghaśaḥ 156
karañjatailamindra luptaroge
karañjaścitrako jātī karavīraśca pācitam
tailamebhirdrutaṃ hanyādabhyaṅgādindra luptakam 157
nīlikādyaṃ tailaṃ keśakalpādau
nīlikā ketakīkandaṃ bhṛṅgarājaḥ kuraṇṭakaḥ
tathā'junasya puṣpāṇi bījakātkusumānyapi 158
kṛṣṇāstilāśca tagaraṃ samūlaṃ kamalaṃ tathā
ayorajaḥ priyaṅguśca dāḍimatvagguḍūcikā 159
triphalā padmapaṅkaśca kalkairebhiḥ pṛthakpṛthak
karṣamātraḥ pacettailaṃ triphalākvāthasaṃyutam 160
bhṛṅgarājarasenaiva siddhaṃ keśasthirīkṛtam
akālapalitaṃ hanti dāruṇaṃ copajihvikām 161
bhṛṅgarājatailamakālapalitādau
bhṛṅgarājarasenaiva lohakiṭṭaṃ phalatrikam
sārivāṃ ca pacetkalkaistailaṃ dāruṇanāśanam
akālapalitaṃ kaṇḍūmindra luptaṃ ca nāśayet 162
irimedādyaṃ tailaṃ mukhadantarogādau
irimedatvacaṃ kṣuṇṇāṃ pacecchatapalonmitām
jaladro ṇetataḥ kvāthaṃ gṛhṇīyātpādaśeṣitam 163
tailasyārddhāḍhakaṃ dattvā kalkaiḥ karṣamitaiḥ pacet
irimedalavaṅgābhyāṃ gairikāgarupadmakaiḥ 164
mañjiṣṭhālodhramadhukairlākṣānyagrodhamustakaiḥ
tvagjātīphalakarpūrakaṅkolakhadiraistathā 165
pataṅgadhātakīpuṣpasūkṣmailānāgakeśaraiḥ
kaṭphalena ca saṃsiddhaṃ tailaṃ mukharujaṃ jayet 166
praduṣṭamāṃsaṃ palitaṃ śīrṇadantaṃ ca sauṣiram
śyāvadantaṃ praharṣaṃ ca vidra dhiṃ kṛmidantakam 167
dantasphuṭaṃ ca daurgandhyaṃ jihvātālvoṣṭhajāṃ rujam 168
jātyāditailaṃ nāḍīvraṇādau
jātīnimbapaṭolānāṃ naktamālasya pallavāḥ
sikthaṃ samadhukaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ dveniśe kaṭurohiṇī 169
mañjiṣṭhāpadmakaṃ lodhramabhayā nīlamutpalam
tutthakaṃ sārivābījaṃ naktamālasya dāpayet 170
etāni samabhāgāni piṣṭvā tailaṃ vipācayet
nāḍīvraṇe samutpanne sphoṭake kacchurogiṣu 171
sadyaḥ śastraprahāreṣu dagdhaviddheṣu caiva hi
nakhadantakṣate dehe vraṇe duṣṭe praśasyate 172
hiṅgvāditailaṃ karṇaśūle
hiṅgutumburuśuṇṭhībhiḥ kaṭutailaṃ vipācayet
tasya pūraṇamātreṇa karṇaśūlaṃ praṇaśyati 173
bilvāditailaṃ bādhirye
bālabilvāni gomūtre piṣṭvā tailaṃ vipācayet
sājakṣīraṃ ca nīraṃ ca bādhiryaṃ hanti pūraṇāt 174
kṣāratailaṃ karṇarogeṣu
bālamūlakaśimbīnāṃ kṣāraḥ kṣārayugaṃ tathā
lavaṇāni ca pañcaiva hiṅguśigru mahauṣadham 175
devadāru vacā kuṣṭhaṃ śatapuṣpā rasāñjanam
granthikaṃ bhadra mustaṃ ca kalkaiḥ karṣamitaiḥ pṛthak 176
tailaprasthaṃ ca vipacetkadalībījapūrayoḥ
rasābhyāṃ madhuśuktena cāturguṇyamitena ca 177
pūyasrāvaṃ karṇanādaṃ śūlaṃ badhiratāṃ kṛmīn
anyāṃśca karṇajānrogānmukharogāṃśca nāśayet 178
madhuśuktam
jambīrāṇāṃ phalarasaḥ prasthaikaḥ kuḍavonmitam
mākṣikaṃ tatra dātavyaṃ palaikā pippalī smṛtā 179
etadekīkṛtaṃ sarvaṃ mṛdbhāṇḍe ca nidhāpayet
yavāmbho madhusaṃyuktaṃ śṛṅgaveraguḍānvitam
dhānyarāśau trirātrasthaṃ madhuśuktamudāhṛtam 180
pāṭhāditailaṃ pīnase
pāṭhā dve ca niśe mūrvā pippalī jātipallavaiḥ
dantyā ca tailaṃ saṃsiddhaṃ nasyaṃ syādduṣṭapīnase 181
vyāghrītailaṃ pīnase
vyāghrīdantīvacāśigrutulasīvyoṣasaindhavaiḥ
kalkaśca pācanaṃ tailaṃ pūtināsāgadāpaham 182
kuṣṭhāditailaṃ chikkāyāṃ
kuṣṭhaṃbilvakaṇāśuṇṭhīdrā kṣākalkakaṣāyavat
sādhitaṃ tailamājyaṃ vā nasyātkṣavathunāśanam 183
gṛhadhūmatailaṃ nāsārśasi
gṛhadhūmakaṇādārukṣāranaktāhvasaindhavaiḥ
siddhaṃ śikharibījaiśca tailaṃ nāsārśasāṃ hitam 184
vajratailaṃ kuṣṭhādau
vajrīkṣīraṃ ravikṣīraṃ dra vaṃ dhattūracitrajam 185
mahiṣīviḍbhavaṃ drā vaṃ sarvāṃśaṃ tilatailakam
pacettailāvaśeṣaṃ ca gomūtre'tha caturguṇe 186
tailāvaśeṣaṃ paktvā ca tattailaṃ prasthamātrakam
gandhakāgniśilātālaṃ viḍaṅgātiviṣāviṣam 187
tiktakośātakīkuṣṭhavacāmāṃsīkaṭutrayam
pītadāru ca yaṣṭyāhvaṃ svarjikākṣārajīrakam 188
devadāru ca karṣāṃśaṃ cūrṇaṃ taile vinikṣipet
vajratailamidaṃ khyātamabhyaṅgātsarvakuṣṭhanut 189
karavīratailaṃ lomaśātanārtham
karavīraśiphādantī trivṛtkośātakī phalam
rambhākṣārodake tailaṃ praśastaṃ lomaśātanam 190
candanāditailaṃ kṣayarogādau
candanāmbunakhairyāmyaṃ yaṣṭīśaileyapadmakam

anuktamānāriṣṭeṣu jalādimānavyavasthā

athāriṣṭāḥ
tatrādau mṛdīkāriṣṭo'śa prabhṛtirogeṣu

ūrustambhāśmarīmehānpratyaṣṭhīlābhagandarān

devadārvādyariṣṭaḥ pramehādau

majjiṣṭhendra yavā dantī tagaraṃ rajanīdvayam 53
rāsnā kṛmighnaṃ mustaṃ ca śirīṣaṃ khadirārjunau

dro ṇaśeṣe kaṣāye ca pūte śīte pradāpayet
dhātakyāḥ ṣoḍaśapalaṃ mākṣikasya tulātrayam 56
vyoṣasya dvipalaṃ dadyāt trijātasya catuṣpalam

jātīphalāni kaṅkolamelātvakpatrakeśaram 67
lavaṅgaṃ maricaṃ caiva palikānyupakalpayet
māsaṃ bhāṇḍe sthitastveṣa babbūlāriṣṭako jayet

drā kṣā'riṣṭaḥ puṣṭyādau
drā kṣātulā'ddha dvidro ṇe jalasya vipacet sudhīḥ
pādaśeṣe kaṣāye ca pūte śīte vinikṣipet 69
guḍasya dvitulāṃ tatra tvagelāpatrakeśaram
priyaṅgurmaricaṃ kṛṣṇā viḍaṅgaṃ ceti cūrṇayet 70
pṛthakpalonmitairbhāgaistato bhāṇḍe nidhāpayet

uraḥkṣataṃ kṣayaṃ hanti kāsaśvāsagalāmayān

rohitakāriṣṭo gudajagrahaṇyādau

pañcakolaṃ trijātaṃ ca triphalāṃ ca vinikṣipet 74
cūrṇayitvāpalāṃśena tatobhāṇḍenidhāpayet
māsādūrdhvaṃ ca pibatāṃ gudajā yānti saṃkṣayam 75
grahaṇīpāṇḍuhṛdro gaplīhagulmodarāṇi ca

daśamūlāriṣṭo vātādau
parṇyau bṛhatyau gokaṇṭo bilvo'gnimanthako'raluḥ 77
pāṭalā kāśmarī ceti daśamūlamihocyate

pañcaviṃśatpalaṃ kuryāccitrakaṃ pauṣkaraṃ tathā
kuryādviṃśapalaṃ lodhraṃ guḍūcī tatsamā bhavet 79
palaiḥ ṣoḍaśabhirdhātrī ravisaṅkhyairdurālabhā

aṣṭabhirgaṇitaḥ kuṣṭhaṃ mañjiṣṭhā devadāru ca
viḍaṅgaṃ madhukaṃ bhārṅgī kapittho'kṣa punarnavā 81
cavyaṃ māṃsī priyaṅguśca sārivā kṛṣṇajīrakam
trivṛtā reṇukaṃ rāsnā pippalī kramukaḥ śaṭī 82
haridrā śatapuṣpā ca padmakaṃ nāgakeśaram

medā cānyā mahāmedā kākolyau ṛddhivṛddhike
kuryātpṛthagdvipalikānpacedaṣṭaguṇe jale 84
caturthāśaṃ śṛtaṃ nītvā mṛdbhāṇḍe saṃnidhāpayet
catuḥṣaṣṭipalāṃ drā kṣāṃ pacennīre caturguṇe 85
tripādaśeṣaṃ śītaṃ ca pūrvakvāthe śṛtaṃ kṣipet
dvātriṃśatpalikaṃ kṣaudraṃ dadyād guṇacatuḥśatam 86
triṃśatpalāni dhātakyāḥ kaṅkolaṃ jalacandanam
jātīphalaṃ lavaṅgaṃ ca tvagelāpatrakeśaram 87
pippalī ceti saṃcūrṇya bhāgairdvipalikaḥ pṛthak
śāṇamātrāṃ ca kastūrīṃ sarvamekatra nikṣipet 88
bhūmau nikhātayedbhāṇḍaṃ tato jātarasaṃ pibet
katakasya phalaṃ kṣiptvā rasaṃ nirmalatāṃ nayet 89
grahaṇīmaruciṃ śvāsaṃ kāsaṃ gulmaṃ bhagandaram
vātavyādhiṃ kṣayaṃ chardiṃ pāṇḍurogaṃ ca kāmalām 90
kuṣṭhānyarśāṃsi mehāṃśca mandāgnimudarāṇi ca
śarkarāmaśmarīṃ mūtrakṛcchraṃ dhātukṣayaṃ jayet 91
kṛśānāṃ puṣṭijanano vandhyānāṃ garbhadaḥ paraḥ
ariṣṭo daśamūlākhyastejaḥ śukrabalapradaḥ 92
iti śrīśārṅgadharasaṃhitāyāṃ madhyakhaṇḍe āsavāriṣṭādisandhānakalpanā nāma daśamo'dhyāyaḥ
dhātuśodhanamāraṇa
atha dhātuśodhanamāraṇakalpanānāmaikādaśo'dhyāyaḥ
atha dhātūnāṃ samudāyena śodhanamāraṇam
tatrādau dhātūnāṃ saṃkhyā nāmāni ca
svarṇatārāratāmrāṇi nāgavaṅgau ca tīkṣṇakam
dhātavaḥ sapta vijñeyāstatastāñchodhayed budhaḥ 1
dhātuśodhanavidhiḥ
svarṇatārāratāmrāyaḥ patrāṇyagnau pratāpayet
niṣiñcettaptataptāni taile takre ca kāñjike 2
gomūtre ca kulatthānāṃ kaṣāye ca tridhā tridhā
evaṃ svarṇādilohānāṃ viśuddhiḥ samprajāyate 3
nāgavaṅgayoḥ śodhanavidhiḥ
nāgavaṅgau prataptau ca gālitau tau niṣecayet
tridhā tridhā viśuddhiḥ syādra vidugdhena ca tridhā 4
atha svarṇamāraṇam
svarṇācca dviguṇaṃ sūtamamlena saha mardayet
tadgolakasamaṃ gandhaṃ nidadhyādadharottaram 5
golakaṃ ca tato rundhyāccharāvadṛḍhasampuṭe
triṃśadvanopalairdadyātpuṭānyevaṃ caturdaśa
nirutthaṃ jāyate bhasma gandho deyaḥ punaḥ punaḥ 6
svarṇamāraṇe dvitīyo vidhiḥ
kāñcane gālite nāgaṃ ṣoḍaśāṃśena nikṣipet 7
cūrṇayitvā tathā'mlena ghṛṣṭvā kṛtvā ca golakam
golakena samaṃ gandhaṃ dattvā caivādharottaram 8
śarāvasampuṭe dhṛtvā puṭet triṃśadvanopalaiḥ
evaṃ saptapuṭairhema nirutthaṃ bhasma jāyate 9
tṛtīyo vidhiḥ
kāñcanārarasairghṛṣṭvā samasūtakagandhayoḥ
kajjalyā hemapatrāṇi lepayetsamamātrayā 10
kāñcanāratvacaḥ kalko mūṣāyugmaṃ prakalpayet
dhṛtvā tatsaṃpuṭe golaṃ mṛṇmūṣāsaṃpuṭe ca tat 11
nidhāya saṃdhirodhaṃ ca kṛtvā saṃśoṣya gomayaiḥ
vahniṃ kharataraṃ kuryādevaṃ dadyātpuṭatrayam
nirutthaṃ jāyate bhasma sarvakāryeṣu yojayet 12
caturtho vidhiḥ
kāñcanāraprakāreṇa lāṅgalī hanti kāñcanam
jvālāmukhī yathā hanyāttathā hanti manaḥ śilā 13
pañcamo vidhiḥ
śilāsindūrayoścūrṇaṃ samayorarkadugdhakaiḥ 14
saptaiva bhāvanā dadyācchoṣayecca punaḥ punaḥ
tatastu gālite hemni kalko'ya dīyate samaḥ 15
punardhamedatitarāṃ yathā kalko vilīyate
evaṃ velātrayaṃ dadyātkalkaṃ hemamṛtirbhavet 16
ṣaṣṭho vidhiḥ
pārāvatamalairlimpedathavā kukkuṭodbhavaiḥ
hemapatrāṇi teṣāṃ ca pradadyādadharottaram 17
gandhacūrṇaṃ samaṃ dattvā śarāvayugasampuṭe
pradadyātkukkuṭapuṭaṃ pañcabhirgomayopalaiḥ 18
evaṃ navapuṭāndadyāddaśamaṃ ca mahāpuṭam
triṃśadvanopalairdeyaṃ jāyate hemabhasmakam 19
svarṇabhasmaguṇāḥ
suvarṇaṃ ca bhavetsvādu tiktaṃ snigdhaṃ himaṃ guru
buddhividyāsmṛtikaraṃ viṣahāri rasāyanam 20
rajatamāraṇe prathamo vidhiḥ
bhāgaikaṃ tālakaṃ mardyaṃ jambhenāmlena kenacit
tena bhāgatrayaṃ tārapatrāṇi parilepayet 21
dhṛtvā mūṣāpuṭe ruddhvā puṭettriṃśadvanopalaiḥ
samuddhṛtya punastālaṃ dattvā ruddhvā puṭe pacet
evaṃ caturdaśapuṭaistārabhasma prajāyate 22
dvitīyo vidhiḥ
snuhīkṣīreṇa sampiṣṭaṃ mākṣikaṃ tena lepayet 23
tālakasya prakāreṇa tārapatrāṇi buddhimān
puṭeccaturdaśapuṭaistāra bhasma prajāyate 24
āra i!pittalai! māraṇavidhiḥ
arkakṣīreṇa sampiṣṭo gandhakastena lepayet
samenārasya patrāṇi śuddhānyamladra vairmuhuḥ 25
tato mūṣāpuṭe dhṛtvā puṭedgajapuṭena ca
evaṃ puṭadvayenaiva bhasmāraṃ bhavati dhruvam
āravatkāṃsyamapyevaṃ bhasmatāṃ yāti niścitam 26
tāmrapittala kāṃsyamāraṇavidhiḥ
arkakṣīravadājaṃ syātkṣīraṃ nirguṇḍikā tathā
tāmrarītidhvanivadhe samagandhakayogataḥ 27
tāmramāraṇavidhiḥ
sūkṣmāṇi tāmrapatrāṇi kṛtvā saṃsvedayed budhaḥ
vāsaratrayamamlena tataḥ khalve vinikṣipet 28
pādāṃśaṃ sūtakaṃ dattvā yāmamamlena mardayet
tata uddhṛtya patrāṇi lepayed dviguṇena ca 29
gandhakenāmlaghṛṣṭena tasya kuryācca golakam
tataḥ piṣṭvā ca mīnākṣīṃ cāṅgerīṃ vā punarnavām 30
tatkalkena bahirgolaṃ lepayedaṅgulonmitam
dhṛtvā tadgolakaṃ bhāṇḍe śarāveṇa ca rodhayet 31
vālukābhiḥ prapūryātha vibhūtilavaṇāṃbubhiḥ
dattvā bhāṃḍamukhe mudrā ṃ! tataśculyāṃ vipācayet 32
kramavṛddhāgninā samyagyāvadyāma catuṣṭayam
svāṅgaśītalamuddhṛtya mardayetsūraṇadra vaiḥ 33
dinaikaṃ golakaṃ kuryādarddhagandhena lepayet
saghṛtena tato mūṣāṃ puṭe gajapuṭe pacet 34
svāṅgaśītaṃ samuddhṛtya mṛtaṃ tāmraṃ śubhaṃ bhavet
vāṃtiṃ bhrāṃtiṃ klamaṃ mūrcchāṃ na karoti kadācana 35
sīsakamāraṇe prathamo vidhiḥ
tāmbūlīrasasampiṣṭaśilālepātpunaḥ punaḥ
dvātriṃśadbhiḥ puṭairnāgo niruttho yāti bhasmatām 36
dvitīyo vidhiḥ
aśvatthaciñcātvakcūrṇaṃ caturthāṃśena nikṣipet
mṛtpātre drā vite nāge lohadarvyā pracālayet 37
yāmaikena bhavedbhasma tattulyāṃ ca manaḥśilām
kāñjikena dvayaṃ piṣṭvā paced dṛḍhapuṭena ca 38
svāṅgaśītaṃ punaḥ piṣṭvā śilayā kāñjikena ca
punaḥ puṭeccharāvābhyāmevaṃ ṣaṣṭipuṭairmṛtiḥ 39
vaṅgamāraṇavidhiḥ
mṛtpātre drā vite vaṅge ciñcā'śvatthatvaco rajaḥ 40
kṣiptvā tena caturthāṃśamayodarvyā pracālayet
tato dviyāmamātreṇa vaṅgabhasma prajāyate 41
atha bhasmasamaṃ tālaṃ kṣiptvā'mlena pramardayet
tato gajapuṭe paktvā punaramlena mardayet 42
tālena daśamāṃśena yāmamekaṃ tataḥ puṭet
evaṃ daśapuṭaiḥ pakvo vaṅgastu mriyate dhruvam 43
lauhamāraṇe prathamo vidhiḥ
śuddhaṃ lohabhavaṃ cūrṇaṃ pātālagaruḍīrasaiḥ
mardayitvā puṭedvahnau dadyādevaṃ puṭatrayam 44
puṭatrayaṃ kumāryā ca kuṭhāracchinnikārasaiḥ
puṭaṣaṭkaṃ tato dadyādevaṃ tīkṣṇamṛtirbhavet 45
dvitīyo vidhiḥ
kṣipettu dvādaśāṃśena daradaṃ tīkṣṇacūrṇataḥ
mardayetkanyakādrā vairyāmayugmaṃ tataḥ puṭet 46
evaṃ saptapuṭairmṛtyuṃ lohacūrṇamavāpnuyāt
rasaiḥ kuṭhāracchinnāyāḥ pātālagaruḍīrasaiḥ
stanyena cārkadugdhena tīkṣṇasyaivaṃ mṛtirbhavet 47
tṛtīyo vidhiḥ
sūtakād dviguṇaṃ gandhaṃ datvā kuryācca kajjalīm
dvayoḥ samaṃ lohacūrṇaṃ mardayetkanyakādra vaiḥ 48
yāmayugmaṃ tataḥ piṇḍaṃ kṛtvā tāmrasya pātrake
gharme dhṛtvā rubūkasya patrairācchādayed budhaḥ 49
yāmārddhenoṣṇatāṃ yāyāddhānyarāśau nyasettataḥ
dattvopari śarāvaṃ tu tridinānte samuddharet 50
piṣṭvā ca gālayedvastrādevaṃ vāritaraṃ bhavet
evaṃ sarvāṇi lohāni svarṇādīnyapi mārayet 51
sarvadhātūnāṃ māraṇe sāmānyavidhiḥ
śilāgandhārkadugdhāktāḥ svarṇādyāḥ sarvadhātavaḥ
mriyante dvādaśapuṭaiḥ satyaṃ guruvaco yathā 52
athopadhātūnāṃ śodhanamāraṇaprakārāḥ
upadhātūnāṃ gaṇanā
mākṣikaṃ tutthakābhrau ca nīlāñjanaśilālakāḥ
rasakañceti vijñeyā ete saptopadhātavaḥ 53
svarṇamākṣikasya śodhanavidhiḥ
mākṣikasya trayo bhāgā bhāgaikaṃ saindhavasya ca
mātuluṅgadra vairvā'tha jambīrotthadra vaiḥ pacet 54
cālayellohaje pātre yāvatpātraṃ sulohitam
bhavettaptaṃ tu saṃśuddhiṃ svarṇamākṣikamṛcchati 55
tasya māraṇavidhiḥ
kulatthasya kaṣāyeṇa ghṛṣṭvā tailena vā puṭet
takreṇa vājamūtreṇa mriyate svarṇamākṣikam 56
raupyamākṣikaśodhanavidhiḥ
karkoṭīmeṣaśṛṅgyutthairdra vairjambīrajairdinam
bhāvayedātape tīvre vimalā śuddhyti dhruvam 57
tutthaśodhanavidhiḥ
viṣṭhayā mardayet tutthaṃ mārjārakakapotayoḥ 58

puṭaṃ dadhnā tathā kṣaudrai rdeyaṃ tutthaviśuddhaye 59
abhrakaśodhanavidhiḥ
kṛṣṇābhrakaṃ dhamedvahnau tataḥ kṣīre vinikṣipet
bhinnapatraṃ tu tatkṛtvā taṇḍulīyāmlayordra vaiḥ
bhāvayedaṣṭayāmaṃ tadevaṃ śuddhyti cābhrakam 60
abhrakamāraṇe prathamo vidhiḥ
kṛtvā dhānyābhrakaṃ tattu śoṣayitthā'tha mardayet

veṣṭayedarkapatraiśca samyaggajapuṭe pacet
punarmardyaṃ punaḥ pācyaṃ saptavāraṃ prayatnataḥ 62
tato vaṭajaṭākvāthaistadvaddeyaṃ puṭatrayam
mriyate nātra saṃdehaḥ sarvayogeṣu yojayet 63
mṛtābhrakasyāmṛtīkaraṇavidhiḥ
tulyaṃ ghṛtaṃ mṛtābhreṇa lohapātre vipācayet
ghṛte jīrṇe tadabhraṃ tu sarvayogeṣu yojayet 64
mṛtābhrakaguṇāḥ
mṛtaṃ tvabhraṃ harenmṛtyuṃ jarāpalitanāśanam
anupānaiśca saṃyuktaṃ tattadro gaharaṃ param 65
abhrakamāraṇe dvitīyo vidhiḥ
śuddhaṃ dhānyābhrakaṃ mustaśuṇṭhīṣaḍbhāgayojitam
mardayetkāñjikenaiva dinaṃ citrakajai rasaiḥ 66
tato gajapuṭaṃ dadyāttasmāduddhṛtya mardayet
triphalāvāriṇā tadvatpuṭedevaṃ puṭaistribhiḥ 67
valāgomūtramusalītulasīsūraṇadra vaiḥ
marditaṃ puṭitaṃ vahnau tritrivelaṃ vrajenmṛtim 68
abhrakasattvaprakāraḥ
dhānyābhrakasya bhāgaikaṃ dvau bhāgau ṭaṅkaṇasya ca 69
piṣṭvā tadandhamūṣāyāṃ ruddhvā tīvrāgninā pacet
svabhāvaṃ śītalaṃ cūrṇaṃ sarvarogeṣu yojayet 70
nīlāñjanaśodhanavidhiḥ
nīlāñjanaṃ cūrṇayitvā jambīradra vabhāvitam
dinaikamātape śuddhaṃ bhavetkāryeṣu yojayet 71
gairikakāsīsādiśodhanavidhiḥ
evaṃ gairikakāsīsaṭaṅkaṇāni varāṭikā

manaḥ śilāśodhanavidhiḥ
pacettryahamajāmūtrairdolāyantre manaḥ śilām

haritālaśodhanavidhiḥ
tālakaṃ kaṇaśaḥ kṛtvā taccūrṇaṃ kāñjike kṣipet

tilataile pacedyāmaṃ yāmaṃ ca triphalājalaiḥ
evaṃ yantre caturyāmaṃ pācyaṃ śuddhyti tālakam 75
rasakakhirpira śodhanavidhiḥ
nṛmūtrevā'tha gomūtre saptāhaṃ rasakaṃ pacet
dolāyantreṇa śuddhiḥ syāttataḥ kāryeṣu yojayet 76
dhātūnāṃ sattvapātanavidhiḥ
lākṣāmīnapayaśchāgaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ mṛgaśṛṅgakam
piṇyākaṃ sarṣapāḥ śigrurguñjorṇāguḍasaindhavāḥ
yavatiktā ghṛtaṃ kṣaudraṃ yathālābhaṃ vicūrṇayet 77
ebhirvimiśritāḥ sarve dhātavo gāḍhavahninā
mūṣādhmātāḥ prajāyante muktasattvā na saṃśayaḥ 78
atha ratnānāṃ śodhanamāraṇaprakārāḥ
tatrādau vajraśodhanavidhiḥ
kulatthakodra vakvāthairdolāyantre vipācayet
vyāghrīkandagataṃ vajraṃ tridinaṃ śuddhimṛcchati 79
vajraśodhane dvitīyo vidhiḥ
taptaṃ taptaṃ tu tadvajraṃ kharamūtrairniṣecayet
punastapyaṃ punaḥ secyamevaṃ kuryāttrisaptadhā 80
vajramāraṇe prathamo vidhiḥ
matkuṇaistālakaṃ piṣṭvā yāvadbhavati golakam
tadgole nihitaṃ vajraṃ tadgolaṃ vahninā dhamet 81
siñcayedaśvamūtreṇa tadgole ca kṣipetpunaḥ
ruddhvā dhmātaṃ punaḥ secyamevaṃ kuryāttrisaptadhā 82
evaṃ ca mriyate vajraṃ cūrṇaṃ sarvatra yojayet 83
dvitīyo vidhiḥ
hiṅgusaindhavasaṃyukte kvāthe kaulatthaje kṣipet
taptaṃ taptaṃ punarvajraṃ bhūyāccūrṇaṃ trisaptadhā 84
tṛtīyo vidhiḥ
maṇḍūkaṃ kāṃsyaje pātre nigṛhya sthāpayetsudhīḥ
sa bhīto mūtrayettatra tanmūtre vajramāvapet
taptaṃ taptaṃ ca bahudhā vajrasyaivaṃ mṛtirbhavet 85
vaikrāntasya saṃśodhanamāraṇavidhiḥ
vaikrāntaṃ vajravacchoddhyaṃ nīlaṃ vā lohitaṃ tathā
hayamūtreṇa tatsecyaṃ taptaṃ taptaṃ dvisaptadhā 86
tatastu meṣaśṛṅgyuktapañcāṅge golake kṣipet
puṭenmūṣāpuṭe ruddhvā kuryādevaṃ ca saptadhā 87
vaikrāntaṃ bhasmatāṃ yāti vajrasthāne niyojayet 88
avaśiṣṭaratnānāṃ śodhanavidhiḥ
svedayeddolikāyantre jayantyāḥ svarasena ca
maṇimuktāpravālānāṃ yāmakaṃ śodhanaṃ bhavet 89
teṣāmeva māraṇavidhiḥ
kumāryāstaṇḍulīyena stanyena ca niṣecayet

mauktikāni pravālāni tathā ratnānyaśeṣataḥ
kṣaṇādvividhavarṇāni mriyante nātra saṃśayaḥ 91
uktamākṣikavanmuktāḥ pravālāni ca mārayet
vajravatsarvaratnāni śodhayenmārayettathā 92
śilājatunaḥ prathamaḥ śodhanavidhiḥ
śilājatu samānīya grīṣmataptaśilācyutam
godugdhaistriphalākvāthairbhṛṅgadrā vaiśca mardayet

dvitīyo vidhiḥ
mukhyāṃ śilājatuśilāṃ sūkṣmakhaṇḍaprakalpitām
nikṣipyātyuṣṇapānīye yāmaikaṃ sthāpayetsudhīḥ 94
mardayitvā tato nīraṃ gṛhṇīyādvastragālitam
sthāpayitvā ca mṛtpātre dhārayedātape budhaḥ 95
uparisthaṃ ghanaṃ yatsyāttatkṣipedanyapātrake
dhārayedātape dhīmānuparisthaṃ ghanaṃ nayet 96
evaṃ punaḥ punarnītvā dvimāsābhyāṃ śilājatu
bhūyātkāryakṣamaṃ vahnau kṣiptaṃ liṅgopamaṃ bhavet 97
nirdhūmaṃ ca tataḥ śuddhaṃ sarvakarmasu yojayet
adhaḥ sthitaṃ ca yaccheṣaṃ tasminnīraṃ vinikṣipet 98
vimardya dhārayedgharme pūrvavaccaiva tannayet 99
maṇḍūrasya śodhanamāraṇavidhiḥ
akṣāṅgārairdhametkiṭṭaṃ lohajaṃ tadgavāṃ jalaiḥ

cūrṇayitvā tataḥ kvāthairdviguṇaistriphalābhavaiḥ
āloḍya bharjayedvahnau maṇḍūraṃ jāyate varam 101
kṣārakalpanā
kṣīravṛkṣasya kāṣṭhāni śuṣkāṇyagnau pradīpayet
nītvā tadbhasma mṛtpātre kṣiptvā nīre caturguṇe
vimardya dhārayedrā trau prātaracchaṃ jalaṃ nayet 102
tannīraṃ kvāthayedvahnau yāvatsarvaṃ viśuṣyati
tataḥ pātrātsamullikhya kṣāro grāhyaḥ sitaprabhaḥ 103
cūrṇābhaḥ pratisāryaḥ syātpeyaḥ syātkvāthavatsthitaḥ
iti kṣāradvayaṃ dhīmānuktakāryeṣu yojayet 104
iti śrīśārṅgadharasaṃhitāyāṃ madhyakhaṇḍe dhātuśodhanamāraṇanāmaikādaśo'dhyāyaḥ
rasādiśodhanamāraṇakalpanā
atha rasādiśodhanamāraṇakalpanānāma dvādaśo'dhyāyaḥ
tatrādau rasapraśaṃsā

sujñena sādhitaḥ kuryātsaṃsiddhiṃ dehalohayoḥ 1
rasanāmāni
rasendra ḥ! pāradaḥ sūto harajaḥ sūtako rasaḥ
miśrakaśceti nāmāni jñeyāni rasakarmasu 2
tāmrādidhātūnāṃ sūryādīnāmadhiṣṭhānanirdeśaḥ
tāmratārāranāgāśca hemavaṅgau ca tīkṣṇakam
kāṃsyakaṃ kāntalohaṃ ca dhātavo nava ye smṛtāḥ
sūryādīnāṃ grahāṇāṃ te kathitā nāmabhiḥ kramāt 3
pāradaśodhanavidhiḥ
rājīrasonamūṣāyāṃ rasaṃ kṣiptvā vibandhayet
vastreṇa dolikāyantre svedayetkāñjikaistr! yaham 4
dinaikaṃ mardayetsūtaṃ kumārīsambhavairdra vaiḥ
tathā citrakajaiḥ kvāthairmardayedekavāsaram 5

triphalāyāstathā kvāthai raso mardyaḥ prayatnataḥ 6
tatastebhyaḥ pṛthakkuryātsūtaṃ prakṣālya kāñjikaiḥ
tataḥ kṣiptvā rasaṃ khalve rasādarddhaṃ ca saindhavam 7
mardayennimbukarasairdinamekamanāratam
tato rājī rasonaśca mukhyaśca navasādaraḥ 8
etai rasasamaistadvatsūto mardyastuṣāmbunā
tataḥ saṃśodhya cakrābhaṃkṛtvā liptvā ca hiṅgunā 9
dvisthālīsampuṭe dhṛtvā pūrayellavaṇena ca
adhaḥ sthālyāṃ tato mudrā ṃ! dadyād dṛḍhatarāṃ budhaḥ 10
viśoṣyāgniṃ vidhāyādho niṣiñcedambunopari

evaṃ nipātayedūrdhvaṃraso doṣavivarjitaḥ

gandhakaśodhanavidhiḥ
lohapātre vinikṣipya ghṛtamagnau pratāpayet

vidrutaṃ gandhakaṃ jñātvā dugdhamadhye viniḥkṣipet

hiṅgulaśodhanavidhiḥ

kālakūṭo vatsanābhaḥ śṛṅgakaśca pradīpakaḥ
hālāhalo brahmaputro hāridra ḥ! saktukastathā 18
saurāṣṭrika iti proktā viṣabhedā amī nava
arkasehuṇḍadhattūralāṅgalīkaravīrakam 19
guñjā'hiphenāvityetāḥ saptopaviṣajātayaḥ
etairvimarditaḥ sūtaśchinnapakṣaḥ prajāyate
mukhaṃ ca jāyate tasya dhātūṃśca grasate kṣaṇāt 20
dvitīyo rasamukhakaraṇavidhiḥ
athavā trikaṭukṣārau rājī lavaṇapañcakam 21
rasono navasāraśca śigruścaikatra cūrṇitaiḥ
samāṃśaiḥ pāradādetairjambīreṇa dra veṇa vā
nimbutoyaiḥ kāñjikairvā soṣṇakhalve vimardayet 22
ahorātratrayeṇa syādra se dhātucaraṃ mukham
tṛtīyo rasamukhakaraṇavidhiḥ
athavā bindulīkīṭai raso mardyastrivāsaram
lavaṇāmlairmukhaṃ tasya jāyate dhātughasmaram 23
kacchapayantreṇa gandhakajāraṇavidhiḥ
atha kacchapayantreṇa gandhajāraṇamucyate
mṛtkuṇḍe nikṣipettoyaṃ tanmadhye ca śarāvakam 24
mahatkuṇḍapidhānābhaṃ madhye mekhalayā yutam
liptvā ca mekhalāmadhyaṃ cūrṇenātra rasaṃ kṣipet 25
rasasyopari gandhasya rajo dadyātsamāṃśakam
dattvopari śarāvaṃ ca bhasmamudrā ṃ! pradāpayet 26
tasyopari puṭaṃ dadyāccaturbhirgomayopalaiḥ
evaṃ punaḥ punargandhaṃ ṣaḍguṇaṃ jārayed budhaḥ 27
gandhe jīrṇe bhavetsūtastīkṣṇāgniḥ sarvakarmakṛt 28
pāradamāraṇavidhiḥ
dhūmasāraṃ rasaṃ torīṃ gandhakaṃ navasādaram 29
yāmaikaṃ mardayedamlairbhāgaṃ kṛtvā samāṃśakam
kācakūpyāṃ vinikṣipya tāṃ ca mṛdvastramudra yā 30
vilipya parito vaktre mudrā ṃ! dattvā ca śoṣayet
adhaḥ sacchidra piṭharīmadhye kūpīṃ niveśayet 31
piṭharīṃ vālukāpūrairbhṛtvā cākūpikāgalam
niveśya cullyāṃ tadadhaḥ kuryādvahniṃ śanaiḥ śanaiḥ 32
tasmādapyadhikaṃ kiñcitpāvakaṃ jvālayetkramāt
evaṃ dvādaśabhiryāmairmriyate sūtakottamaḥ 33
sphoṭayetsvāṅgaśītaṃ tamūrdhvagaṃ gandhakaṃ tyajet
adhaḥ sthaṃ mṛtasūtaṃ ca sarvakarmasu yojayet 34
dvitīyaḥ pāradamāraṇavidhiḥ
apāmārgasya bījānāṃ mūṣāyugmaṃ prakalpayet
tatsampuṭe nyasetsūtaṃ malayūdugdhamiśritam 35
dro ṇapuṣpīprasūnāni viḍaṅgamirimedakaḥ
etaccūrṇamadhordhvaṃ ca dattvā mudrā pradīyate 36
taṃ golaṃ sandhayetsamyaṅmṛṇmūṣāsampuṭe sudhīḥ
mudrā ṃ! dattvā śodhayitvā tato gajapuṭe pacet
evamekapuṭenaiva jāyate bhasma sūtakam 37
tṛtīyaḥ pāradamāraṇavidhiḥ
kākodumbarikādugdharasaṃ kiñcidvimardayet 38
taddugdhaghṛṣṭahiṅgośca mūṣāyugmaṃ prakalpayet
kṣiptvā tatsampuṭe sūtaṃ tatra mudrā ṃ! pradāpayet 39
dhṛtvā taṃ golakaṃ prājño mṛṇmūṣāsampuṭe'dhike
pacenmṛdupuṭenaiva sūtako yāti bhasmatām 40
caturthaḥ pāradamāraṇavidhiḥ
nāgavallīrasairghṛṣṭaḥ karkoṭīkandagarbhitaḥ
mṛṇmūṣāsampuṭe paktvā sūto yātyeva bhasmatām 41
atha jvarādau rasāḥ procyante
tatra jvarāṅkuśarasaḥ
khaṇḍitaṃ hariṇaśṛṅgaṃ jvālāmukhyā rasaiḥ samam
ruddhvā bhāṇḍe paceccullyāṃ yāmayugmaṃ tato nayet 42
aṣṭāṃśaṃ trikaṭuṃ dadyānniṣkamātraṃ ca bhakṣayet
nāgavallīrasaiḥ sārddhaṃ vātapittajvarāpaham 43
ayaṃ jvarāṅkuśo nāma rasaḥ sarvajvarāpahaḥ
ekāhikaṃ dvyāhikaṃ ca tr! yāhikaṃ vā na saṃśayaḥ 44
jvarārirasaḥ
pāradaṃ rasakaṃ tālaṃ tutthaṃ ṭaṅkaṇagandhake
sarvametatsamaṃ śuddhaṃ kāravellīrasairdinam 45
mardayellepayettena tāmrapātrodaraṃ bhiṣak
aṅgulyardhapramāṇena tato ruddhvā ca tanmukham 46
pacettaṃ vālukāyantre kṣiptvā dhānyāni tanmukhe
yadā sphuṭanti dhānyāni tadā siddhaṃ vinirdiśet 47
tato nayetsvāṅgaśītaṃ tāmrapātrodarādbhiṣak
rasaṃ jvarārināmānaṃ vicūrṇya maricaiḥ samam 48
māṣaikaṃ parṇakhaṇḍena bhakṣayennāśayejjvaram
tridinairviṣamaṃ tīvramekadvitricaturthakam 49
śītajvarārirasaḥ
tālakaṃ tutthakaṃ tāmraṃ rasaṃ gandhaṃ manaḥśilām
karṣaṃ karṣaṃ prayoktavyaṃ mardayet triphalā'mbubhiḥ 50
golaṃ nyasetsampuṭake puṭaṃ dadyāt prayatnataḥ
tato nītvā'kadugdhena vajrīdugdhena saptadhā 51
kvāthena dantyāḥ śyāmāyāḥ bhāvayetsaptadhā punaḥ
māṣamātraṃ rasaṃ divyaṃ pañcāśanmaricairyutam 52
guḍaṃ gadyāṇakaṃ caiva tulasīdalayugmakam
bhakṣayettridinaṃ bhaktyā śītārirdurlabhaḥ paraḥ 53
pathyaṃ dugdhaudanaṃ deyaṃ viṣamaṃ śītapūrvakam
dāhapūrvaṃ haratyāśu tṛtīyakacaturthakau
dvyāhikaṃ satataṃ caiva vaivarṇyaṃ ca niyacchati 54
jvaraghnī guṭikā
bhāgaikaḥ syādra sācchuddhādaileyaḥ pippalī śivā
ākārakarabho gandhaḥ kaṭutailena śodhitaḥ 55
phalāni cendra vāruṇyāścaturbhāgamitā amī
ekatra mardayeccūrṇamindra vāruṇikārasaiḥ 56
māṣonmitāṃ guṭīṃ kṛtvā dadyātsadyojvare budhaḥ
chinnārasānupānena jvaraghnī guṭikā matā 57
kṣayādau lokanātharasaḥ
śuddho bubhukṣitaḥ sūto bhāgadvayamito bhavet
tathā gandhasya bhāgau dvau kuryātkajjalikāṃ tayoḥ 58
sūtāccaturguṇeṣveva kapardeṣu vinikṣipet
bhāgaikaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ dattvā gokṣīreṇa vimardayet 59
tathā śaṅkhasya khaṇḍānāṃ bhāgānaṣṭau prakalpayet
kṣipetsarvaṃ puṭasyāntaścūrṇaliptaśarāvayoḥ 60
gartte hastonmite dhṛtvā puṭedgajapuṭena ca
svāṅgaśītaṃ samuddhṛtya piṣṭvā tatsarvamekataḥ
ṣaḍguñjāsammitaṃ cūrṇamekonatriṃśadūṣaṇaiḥ 61
tatrānupānāni
ghṛtena vātaje dadyānnavanītena pittaje
kṣaudre ṇa śleṣmaje dadyādatīsāre kṣaye tathā 62
arucau grahaṇīroge kārśye mandānale tathā
kāsaśvāseṣu gulmeṣu lokanātharaso hitaḥ 63
tadbhakṣaṇavidhiḥ
tasyopari ghṛtānnaṃ ca bhuñjīta kavalatrayam
mañce kṣaṇaikamuttānaḥ śayītānupadhānake 64
pathyabhojanam
anamlamannaṃ saghṛtaṃ bhuñjīta madhuraṃ dadhi
prāyeṇa jāṅgalaṃ māṃsaṃ pradeyaṃ ghṛtapācitam 65
sudugdhabhaktaṃdadyācca jāte'gnau sāndhyabhojane
saghṛtānmudgavaṭakānvyañjaneṣvavacārayet 66
tatra snānavyavasthā
tilāmalakakalkena snāpayetsarpiṣā'thavā
abhyañjayetsarpiṣā ca snānaṃ koṣṇodakena ca 67
sevanasamaye tyājyapadārthāḥ
kvacittailaṃ na gṛhṇīyānna bilvaṃ kāravellakam
vārtākaṃ śapharīṃ ciñcāṃ tyajedvyāyāmamaithune 68
madyaṃ sandhānakaṃ hiṅgu śuṇṭhīṃ māṣānmasūrakān
kūṣmāṇḍaṃ rājikāṃ kopaṃ kāñjikaṃ caiva varjayet 69
tyajedayuktanidrā ṃ! ca kāṃsyapātre ca bhojanam
kakārādiyutaṃ sarvaṃ tyajecchākaphalādikam 70
pathyo'ya lokanāthe'stuai! śubhanakṣatravāsare
sevanavidhiḥ
pūrṇātithau site pakṣe jāte candra bale tathā 71
pūjayitvā lokanāthaṃ kumārīṃ bhojayettataḥ
dānaṃ dattvā dvighaṭikāmadhye grāhyo rasottamaḥ 72
rasajadāhaśāntyupāyaḥ
rasāccejjāyate tāpastadā śarkarayā yutam 73
sattvaṃ guḍūcyā gṛhṇīyādvaṃśarocanayā yutam
kharjūraṃ dāḍimaṃ drā kṣāmikṣukhaṇḍāni dāpayet 74
rogānusāreṇānupānāni
arucau nistuṣaṃ dhānyaṃ ghṛtabhṛṣṭaṃ saśarkaram
dadyāttathā jvare dhānyaguḍūcīkvāthamāharet 75
uśīravāsakakvāthaṃ dadyātsamadhuśarkaram
raktapitte kaphe śvāse kāse ca svarasaṃkṣaye 76
agnibhṛṣṭajayācūrṇaṃ madhunā niśi dīyate
nidrā nāśe'tisāre ca grahaṇyāṃ mandapāvake 77
sauvarcalābhayākṛṣṇācūrṇamuṣṇodakaiḥ pibet
śūle'jīrṇe tathā kṛṣṇā madhuyuktā jvare hitā 78
plīhodare vātarakte chardyāṃ caiva gudāṅkure
nāsikādiṣu rakteṣu rasaṃ dāḍimapuṣpajam 79
dūrvāyāḥ svarasaṃ nasye pradadyāccharkarāyutam
kolamajjā kaṇā barhipakṣabhasma saśarkaram 80
madhunā lehayecchardihikkākopasya śāntaye
vidhireṣa prayojyastu sarvasminpoṭṭalīrase 81
mṛgāṅke hemagarbhe ca mauktikākhye raseṣu ca
ityayaṃ lokanāthākhyo rasaḥ sarvarujo jayet 82
laghulokanātharasaḥ
varāṭabhasma maṇḍūraṃ cūrṇayitvā ghṛte pacet
tatsamaṃ māricaṃ cūrṇaṃ nāgavallyāṃ vibhāvitam 83
taccūrṇaṃ madhunā lehyamathavā navanītakaiḥ
māṣamātraṃ kṣayaṃ hanti yāme yāme ca bhakṣitam 84
lokanātharaso hyeṣa maṇḍalādrā jayakṣmanut 85
kṣayādau mṛgāṅkapoṭṭalīrasaḥ
bhūrjavattanupatrāṇi hemnaḥ sūkṣmāṇi kārayet
tulyāni tāni sūtena khalve kṣiptvā vimardayet 86
kāñcanārarasenaiva jvālāmukhyā rasena vā
lāṅgalyā vā rasaistāvadyāvadbhavati piṣṭikā 87
tato hemnaścaturthāṃśaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ tatra nikṣipet
piṣṭamauktikacūrṇaṃ ca hemadviguṇamāvapet 88
teṣu sarvasamaṃ gandhaṃ kṣiptvā caikatra mardayet
teṣāṃ kṛtvā tato golavāsobhiḥ pariveṣṭayet 89
paścānmṛdā veṣṭayitvā śoṣayitvā ca dhārayet
śarāvasampuṭasyāntastatra mudrā ṃ! pradāpayet 90
lavaṇāpūrite bhāṇḍe dhārayettaṃ ca sampuṭam
mudrā ṃ! dattvā śoṣayitvā bahubhirgomayaiḥ puṭet 91
tataḥ śīte samāhṛtya gandhaṃ sūtasamaṃ kṣipet
ghṛṣṭvā ca pūrvavatkhalve puṭed gajapuṭena ca 92
svāṅgaśītaṃ tato nītvā guñjāyugmaṃ prayojayet
aṣṭabhirmaricairyuktaḥ kṛṣṇātrayayuto'thavā 93
vilokya deyo doṣādīnekaikā rasaraktikā
sarpiṣā madhunā vā'pi dadyāddoṣādyapekṣayā 94
lokanāthasamaṃ pathyaṃ kuryātsvasthamanāḥ śuciḥ
śleṣmāṇaṃ grahaṇīṃ kāsaṃ śvāsaṃ kṣayamarocakam
mṛgāṅko'ya raso hanyātkṛśatvaṃ balahīnatām 95
kṣayaśvāsādau hemagarbhapoṭṭalīrasaḥ
sūtātpādapramāṇena hemnaḥ piṣṭaṃ prakalpayet
tayoḥ syād dviguṇo gandho mardayetkāñcanāriṇā 96
kṛtvā golaṃ kṣipenmūṣāsampuṭe mudra yettataḥ
paced bhūdharayantreṇa vāsaratritayaṃ budhaḥ 97
tata uddhṛtya tatsarvaṃ dadyād gandhaṃ ca tatsamam
mardayedārdra karasaiścitrakasvarasena ca 98
sthūlapītavarāṭāṃśca pūrayettena yuktitaḥ
etasmādauṣadhātkuryādaṣṭamāṃśena ṭaṅkaṇam 99
ṭaṅkaṇārdhaṃ viṣaṃ dattvā piṣṭvā sehuṇḍadugdhakaiḥ
mudra yettena kalkena varāṭānāṃmukhāni ca 100
bhāṇḍe cūrṇapralipte'tha dhṛtvā mudrā ṃ! pradāpayet
gartte hastonmite dhṛtvā puṭedgajapuṭena ca 101
svāṅgaśītaṃ rasaṃ jñātvā pradadyāllokanāthavat
pathyaṃ mṛgāṅkavajjñeyaṃ tridinaṃ lavaṇaṃ tyajet 102
yadā chardirbhavettasya dadyācchinnāśṛtaṃ tadā
madhuyuktaṃ tathā śleṣmakope dadyādguḍārdra kam 103
vireke bharjitā bhaṅgā pradeyā dadhisaṃyutā
jayetkāsaṃ kṣayaṃ śvāsaṃ grahaṇīmaruciṃ tathā 104
agniṃ ca kurute dīptaṃ kaphavātaṃ niyacchati
hemagarbha paro jñeyo rasaḥ poṭṭalikā'bhidhaḥ 105
dvitīyo hemagarbhapoṭṭalīrasaḥ
rasasya bhāgāścatvārastāvantaḥ kanakasya ca
tayośca piṣṭikāṃ kṛtvā gandho dvādaśabhāgakaḥ 106
kuryātkajjalikāṃ teṣāṃ muktābhāgāśca ṣoḍaśaḥ
caturviṃśacca śaṅkhasya bhāgaikaṃ ṭaṅkaṇasya ca 107
ekatra mardayetsarvaṃ pakvanimbūkajaiḥ rasaiḥ
kṛtvā teṣāṃ tato golaṃ mūṣāsampuṭake nyaset 108
mudrā ṃ! dattvā tato hastamātre gartte ca gomayaiḥ
puṭed gajapuṭenaiva svāṅgaśītaṃ samuddharet 109
piṣṭvā guñjācaturmānaṃ dadyādgavyājyasaṃyutam
ekonatriṃśadunmānamaricaiḥ saha dīyate 110
rājate mṛṇmaye pātre kācaje vā'valehayet
lokanāthasamaṃ pathyaṃ kuryācca svasthamānasaḥ 111
kāse śvāse kṣaye vāte kaphe grahaṇikāgade
atisāre prayoktavyā poṭṭalī hemagarbhikā 112
viṣamajvarādau mahājvarāṅkuśarasaḥ
śuddhasūto viṣaṃ gandhaḥ pratyekaṃ śāṇasammitāḥ
dhūrttabījaṃ triśāṇaṃ syātsarvebhyo dviguṇā bhavet 113
hemāhvā kārayedeṣāṃ sūkṣmaṃ cūrṇaṃ prayatnataḥ
deyaṃ jambīramajjābhiścūrṇaṃ guñjādvayonmitam 114
ārdra kasvarasairvā'pi jvaraṃ hanti tridoṣajam
ekāhikaṃ dvyāhikaṃ vā tr! yāhikaṃ vā caturthakam 115
viṣamaṃ ca jvaraṃ hanyādvikhyāto'ya jvarāṅkuśaḥ 116
atisārādau ānanda bhairavarasaḥ
daradaṃ vatsanābhaṃ ca maricaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ kaṇām
cūrṇayetsamabhāgena raso hyānandabhairavaḥ 117
guñjaikaṃ vā dviguñjaṃ vā balaṃ jñātvā prayojayet
madhunā lehayeccānu kuṭajasya phalaṃ tvacam 118
cūrṇitaṃ karṣamātraṃ tu tridoṣotthātisārajit
dadhyannaṃ dāpayetpathyaṃ gavājyaṃ takrameva ca
pipāsāyāṃ jalaṃ śītaṃ vijayā ca hitā niśi 119
tridoṣe laghusūcikābharaṇarasaḥ
viṣaṃ palamitaṃ sūtaḥ śāṇikaścūrṇayed dvayam
taccūrṇaṃ sampuṭe kṣiptvā kāvaliptaśarāvayoḥ 120
mudrā ṃ! dattvā ca saṃśoṣya tataśculyāṃ niveśayet
vahniṃ śanaiḥ śanaiḥ kuryātpraharadvayasaṃkhyayā 121
tata udghāṭayenmudrā muparisthāṃ śarāvakāt
saṃlagno yo bhavetsūtastaṃ gṛhṇīyācchanaiḥ śanaiḥ 122
vāyusparśo yathā na syāttathā kupyāṃ niveśayet
yāvatsūcyā mukhe lagnaṃ kupyā niryāti bheṣajam 123
tāvanmātro raso deyo mūrcchite sannipātini
kṣureṇa pracchite mūrdhniṃ tatrāṅgulyā ca gharṣayet 124
raktabheṣajasamparkānmūrcchito'pi hi jīvati
tathaiva sarpadaṣṭastu mṛtāvastho'pi jīvati 125
yadā tāpo bhavettasya madhuraṃ tatra dīyate 126
sannipāte jalabandhurasaḥ
sūtabhasmasamaṃ gandhaṃ gandhātpādaṃ manaḥ śilā
mākṣikaṃ pippalī vyoṣaṃ pratyekaṃ śilayā samam 127
cūrṇayedbhāvayetpittairmatsyamāyūrasambhavaiḥ
saptadhā bhāvayecchuṣkaṃ deyaṃ guñjādvayaṃ hitam 128
tālaparṇīrasaścānu pañcakolaśṛto'thavā
jalabandhuraso nāma sannipātaṃ niyacchati
jalayogaśca karttavyastena vīryaṃ bhavedra se 129
sannipāte pañcavaktrarasaḥ
śuddhasūtaṃ viṣaṃ gandhaṃ maricaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ kaṇām
mardayed dhūrttajadrā vairdinamekaṃ ca śoṣayet 130
pañcavaktro raso nāma dviguñjaḥ sannipātajit
arkamūlakaṣāyaṃ tu satr! yūṣamanupāyayet 131
yuktaṃ dadhyodanaṃ pathyaṃ jalayogaṃ ca kārayet
rasenānena śāmyanti sakṣaudre ṇa kaphādayaḥ 132
madhvārdra karasaṃ cānupibedagnivivṛddhaye
yatheṣṭaṃ ghṛtamāṃsāśī śakto bhavati pāvakaḥ 133
sannipāte unmattarasaḥ
rasaṃ gandhakatulyāṃśaṃ dhattūraphalajadra vaiḥ
mardayeddinamekaṃ tu tattulyaṃ trikaṭuṃ kṣipet 134
unmattākhyo raso nāmnā nasye syātsannipātajit 135
sannipātādau añjanarasaḥ
nistvagjaipālabījaṃ ca daśaniṣkaṃ vicūrṇayet
maricaṃ pippaladyaṃ sūtaṃ pratiniṣkaṃ vimiśrayet 136
bhāvyo jambīrajaidrā rvaiḥ! saptāhaṃ samprayatnataḥ
raso'yamañjane dattaḥ sannipātaṃ vināśayet 137
śūlādau nārācarasaḥ
sūtaṃ ṭaṅkaṇakaṃ tulyaṃ maricaṃ sūtatulyakam
gandhakaṃ pippaladyaṃ śuṇṭhīṃ dvau dvau bhāgau vicūrṇayet 138
sarvatulyaṃ kṣipeddantībījaṃ nistuṣitaṃ bhiṣak
dviguñjaṃ recanaṃ siddhaṃ nārāco'ya mahārasaḥ
ādhmānaṃ malaviṣṭambhānudāvarttaṃ ca nāśayet 139
śūlādau icchābhedīrasaḥ
daradaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ śuṇṭhī pippalī ceti kārṣikāḥ
hemāhvā palamātrā syāddantībījaṃ ca tatsamam 140
vicūrṇyaikatra sarvāṇi godugdhenaiva pāyayet
triguñjaṃ recanaṃ dadyādviṣṭambhādhmānarogiṣu 141
sarvamehe vasantakusumākararasaḥ
dvau bhāgau hemabhūteśca gaganaṃ cāpi tatsamam
lohabhasma trayo bhāgāścatvāro rasabhasmanaḥ 142
vaṅgabhasma tribhāgaṃ syātsarvamekatra mardayet
pravālaṃ mauktikaṃ caiva rasasāmyena dāpayet 143
bhāvanā gavyadugdhena rasairghṛṣṭvā'ṭaruṣakaiḥ
haridrā vāriṇā caiva mocakandarasena ca 144
śatapatrarasenāpi mālatyāḥ svarasena ca
paścānmṛgamadaścandra tulasīrasabhāvitaḥ 145
kusumākara ityeṣa vasantapadapūrvakaḥ
guñjādvayaṃ dadītāsya madhunā sarvamehanut 146
sitācandanasaṃyuktaścāmlapittādirogajit 147
kṣayādau rājamṛgāṅkarasaḥ
sūtabhasma trayo bhāgā bhāgaikaṃ hemabhasmakam
mṛtābhrasya ca bhāgaikaṃ śilāgandhakatālakam 148
pratibhāgadvayaṃ śuddhamekīkṛtya vicūrṇayet
varāṭānpūrayettena chāgīkṣīreṇa ṭaṅkaṇam 149
piṣṭvā tena mukhaṃ ruddhvā mṛdbhāṇḍe sannirodhayet
śuṣkaṃ gajapuṭe paktvā cūrṇayetsvāṅgaśītalam 150
raso rājamṛgāṅko'ya caturguñjaḥ kṣayāpahaḥ
daśapippalikākṣaudrai rekonatriṃśadūṣaṇaiḥ 151
saghṛtaṃ dāpayetpathyaṃ strīkopāgniśramāṃstyajet
pathyaṃ vā laghumāṃsāni rājarogapraśāntaye 152
kṣayaśvāsādau svayamagnirasaḥ
śuddhaṃ sūtaṃ dvidhā gandhaṃ kuryātkhalvena kajjalīm
tayoḥ samaṃ tīkṣṇacūrṇaṃ mardayetkanyakādra vaiḥ 153
dviyāmānte kṛtaṃ golaṃ tāmrapātre vinikṣipet
ācchādyairaṇḍapatreṇa yāmārdhe'tyuṣṇatā bhaved 154
dhānyarāśau nyasetpaścādahorātrātsamuddharet
saṃcūrṇya gālayedvastre satyaṃ vāritaraṃ bhavet 155
bhāvayetkanyakādrā vaiḥ saptadhā bhṛṅgajaistathā
kākamācīkuraṇṭotthadra vairmuṇḍyāḥ punarnavaiḥ 156
sahadevyamṛtānīlīnirguṇḍīcitrajaistathā
saptadhā tu pṛthagdrā vairbhāvyaṃ śoṣyaṃ tathātape 157
siddhayogo hyayaṃ khyātaḥ siddhānāṃ ca mukhāgataḥ
anubhūto mayā satyaṃ sarvarogagaṇāpahaḥ 158
svarṇādīnmārayedevaṃ cūrṇīkṛtya tu lohavat
triphalāmadhusaṃyuktaḥ sarvarogeṣu yojayet 159
trikaṭutriphalailābhirjātīphalalavaṅgakaiḥ
samabhāgonmitairetaiḥ samaḥ pūrvaraso bhavet 160
saṃcūrṇya lehayetkṣaudrai rbhakṣyaṃ niṣkadvayaṃ dvayam
svayamagniraso nāmnā kṣayakāsanikṛntanaḥ 161
sarvakāseṣvamṛtārṇavarasaḥ
pāradaṃ gandhakaṃ śuddhaṃ mṛtalohaṃ ca ṭaṅkaṇam
rāsnā viḍaṅgatriphalā devadāru kaṭutrayam 162
amṛtā padmakaṃ kṣaudraṃ viśvaṃ tulyāṃśacūrṇitam
triguñjaṃ sarvakāsārttaḥ sevayedamṛtārṇavam 163
śvāse sūryāvartto rasaḥ
sūtārdho gandhako mardyo yāmaikaṃ kanyakādra vaiḥ
dvayostulyaṃ tāmrapatraṃ pūrvakalkena lepayet 164
dinaikaṃsthālikāyantre pakvamādāya cūrṇayet
sūryāvartto raso hyeṣa dviguñjaḥ śvāsajidbhavet 165
vātaroge svacchandabhairavarasaḥ
śuddhaṃ sūtaṃ mṛtaṃ lohaṃ tāpyaṃ gandhakatālake
pathyā'gnimanthaṃ nirguḍī tr! yūṣaṇaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ viṣam 166
tulyāṃśaṃ mardayetkhalve dinaṃ nirguṇḍikādra vaiḥ
muṇḍīdrā vairdinaikaṃ tu dviguñjaṃ vaṭakīkṛtam 167
bhakṣayedvātarogārttonāmnā svacchandabhairavam
rāsnā'mṛtādevadāruśuṇṭhīvātārijaṃ śṛtam
sagugguluṃ pibetkoṣṇamanupānaṃ sukhāvaham 168
grahaṇīroge haṃsapoṭṭalīrasaḥ
dagdhānkapardikānpiṣṭvā tr! yūṣaṇaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ viṣam
gandhakaṃ śuddhasūtaṃ ca tulyaṃ jambīrajairdra vaiḥ 169
mardayedbhakṣeyanmāṣaṃ maricājyaṃ lihedanu
nihanti grahaṇīrogaṃ pathyaṃ takraudanaṃ hitam 170
aśmaryāṃ trivikramo rasaḥ
mṛtaṃ tāmramajākṣīraiḥ pācyaṃ tulyairgatadra vam
tattāmraṃ śuddhasūtaṃ ca gandhakaṃ ca samaṃ samam 171
nirguṇḍīsvarasairmardyaṃ tadgolaṃ sandhayeddinam
yāmaikaṃ bālukāyantre pācyaṃ yojyaṃ dviguñjakam 172
bījapūrakamūlaṃ tu sajalaṃ cānupāyayet
rasastrivikramo nāmnā māsaikenāśmarīpraṇut 173
kuṣṭhādau mahātāleśvararasaḥ
tālaṃ tāpyaṃ śilāṃ sūtaṃ śuddhaṃ saindhavaṭaṅkaṇe
samāṃśaṃ cūrṇayetkhalve sūtād dviguṇagandhakam 174
gandhatulyaṃ mṛtaṃ tāmraṃ jambīrairdinapañcakam
mardyaṃ ṣaḍbhiḥ puṭaiḥ pācyaṃ bhūdhare saṃpuṭodare 175
puṭe puṭe dra vairmardyaṃ sarvametattu ṣaṭpalam
dvipalaṃ māritaṃ tāmraṃ lohabhasma catuṣpalam 176
jambīrāmlena tatsarvaṃ dinaṃ mardyaṃ puṭellaghu
triṃśadaṃśaṃ viṣaṃ cāsya kṣiptvā sarvaṃ vicūrṇayet 177
māhiṣājyena saṃmiśraṃ niṣkārdhaṃ bhakṣayetsadā
madhvājyairvākucīcūrṇaṃ karṣamātraṃ lihedanu 178
sarvakuṣṭhāni hantyāśu mahātāleśvaro rasaḥ 179
kuṣṭhakuṭhārarasaḥ
bhasmasūtasamo gandho mṛtāyastāmragugguluḥ
triphalā ca mahānimbaścitrakaścaśilājatu
ityetaccūrṇitaṃ kuryātpratyekaṃ śāṇaṣoḍaśam 180
catuḥṣaṣṭiḥ karañjasya bījacūrṇaṃ prakalpayet
catuḥṣaṣṭimṛtaṃ cābhraṃ madhvājyābhyāṃ viloḍayet 181
snigdhe bhāṇḍe dhṛtaṃ khāded dviniṣkaṃ sarvakuṣṭhanut
rasaḥ kuṣṭhakuṭhāro'ya galatkuṣṭhanivāraṇaḥ 182
śvetakuṣṭhādau udayādityo rasaḥ
śuddhaṃ sūtaṃ dvidhā gandhaṃ mardyaṃ kanyādra vairdinam
tadgolaṃ piṭharīmadhye tāmrapātreṇa rodhayet 183
sūtakād dviguṇenaiva śuddhenādhomukhena ca
pārśve bhasma nidhāyātha pātrordhvaṃ gomayaṃ jalam 184
kiñcit kiñcitpradātavyaṃ cullyāṃ yāmadvayaṃ pacet
caṇḍāgninā taduddhṛtya svāṅgaśītaṃ vicūrṇayet 185
kāṣṭhodumbarikāvahnitriphalārājavṛkṣakam
viḍaṅgaṃ vākucībījaṃ kvāthayettena bhāvayet 186
dinaikamudayādityoraso deyo dviguñjakaḥ
vicarcikāṃ dadrukuṣṭhaṃ vātaraktaṃ ca nāśayet 187
anupānaṃ ca kartavyaṃ vākucīphalacūrṇakam
khadirasya kaṣāyeṇa samena paripācitam 188
triśāṇaṃ tad gavāṃ kṣīraiḥ kvāthairvā traiphalaiḥ pibet
tridinānte bhavetsphoṭaḥ saptāhādvā kilāsake 189
nīladyaṃ guñjāṃ ca kāsīsaṃ dhattūraṃ haṃsapādikām
sūryabhaktāṃ ca cāṅgerīṃ piṣṭvā mūlānilepayet 190
sphoṭasthānapraśāntyarthaṃ saptarātraṃ punaḥ punaḥ
śvetakuṣṭhaṃ nihantyāśu sādhyāsādhyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ 191
aparaḥ śvitralepo'pi kathyate'tra bhiṣagvaraiḥ
guñjāphalāgnicūrṇaṃ ca lepitaṃ śvetakuṣṭhanut 192
śilā'pāmārgabhasmāpi liptaṃ śvitraṃ vināśayet 193
kuṣṭhādau sarveśvararasaḥ
śuddhaṃ sūtaṃ caturgandhaṃ palaṃ yāmaṃ vicūrṇayet
mṛtatāmrābhralohānāṃ daradasya palaṃ palam
suvarṇaṃ rajataṃ caiva pratyekaṃ daśaniṣkakam 194
māṣaikaṃ mṛtavajraṃ ca tālaṃ śuddhaṃ paladvayam
jambīronmattavāsābhiḥ snuhyarka viṣamuṣṭibhiḥ 195
mardyaṃ hayārijaidrā rvaiḥ! pratyekena dinaṃ dinam
evaṃ saptadinaṃ mardyaṃ tadgolaṃ vastraveṣṭitam 196
vālukāyantragaṃ svedyaṃ tridinaṃ laghuvahninā
ādāya cūrṇayecchlakṣṇaṃ palaikaṃ yojayedviṣam 197
dvipalaṃ pippalīcūrṇaṃ miśraṃ sarveśvaro rasaḥ
dviguñjo lihyate kṣaudrai ḥ! suptimaṇḍalakuṣṭhanut 198
vākucīdevakāṣṭhaṃ ca karṣamātraṃ sucūrṇayet
lihedairaṇḍatailāktamanupānaṃ sukhāvaham 199
suptyādau svarṇakṣīrīrasaḥ
hemāhvāṃ pañcapalikāṃ kṣiptvā takraghaṭe pacet
takre jīrṇe samuddhṛtya punaḥ kṣīraghaṭe pacet 200
kṣīre jīrṇe samuddhṛtya kṣālayitvā viśoṣayet
taccūrṇaṃ pañcapalikaṃ maricānāṃ paladvayam 201
palaikaṃ mūrcchitaṃ sūtamekīkṛtya tu bhakṣayet
niṣkaikaṃ suptikuṣṭhārttaḥ svarṇakṣīrīraso hyayam 202
pramehe mehabaddharasaḥ
bhasmasūtaṃ mṛtaṃ kāntaṃ muṇḍabhasma śilājatu
śuddhaṃ tāpyaṃ śilāṃ vyoṣaṃ triphalāṃ kolabījakam 203
kapitthaṃ rajanīcūrṇaṃ bhṛṅgarājena bhāvayet
viṃśadvāraṃ viśoṣyātha madhuyuktaṃ lihetsadā 204
niṣkamātro harenmehānmehabaddho raso mahān
mahānimbasya bījāni piṣṭvā ṣaṭsammitāni ca 205
palataṇḍulatoyena ghṛtaniṣkadvayena ca
ekīkṛtya pibeccānu hanti mehaṃ cirantanam 206
jalodarādau mahāvahnirasaḥ
catuḥsūtasya gandhāṣṭau rajanī triphalā śivā
pratyekaṃ ca dvibhāgaṃ syāttrivṛjjaipālacitrakam 207
pratyekaṃ ca tribhāgaṃ syāt tr! yūṣaṇaṃ dantijīrake
pratyekamaṣṭabhāgaṃ syādekīkṛtya vicūrṇayet 208
jayantīsnukpayobhṛṅgavahnivātāritailakaiḥ
pratyekena kramādbhāvyaṃ saptavāraṃ pṛthakpṛthak 209
mahāvahniraso nāma niṣkamuṣṇajalaiḥ pibet

dinānte dāpayetpathyaṃ varjayecchītalaṃ jalam
sarvodaraharaḥ prokto mūḍhavātaharaḥ paraḥ 211
gulmaplīhādau vidyādhararasaḥ
gandhakaṃ tālakaṃ tāpyaṃ mṛtatāmraṃ manaḥ śilām
śuddhaṃ sūtaṃ ca tulyāṃśaṃ mardayedbhāvayed dinam 212
pippalyāstu kaṣāyeṇa vajrīkṣīreṇa bhāvayet
niṣkārdhaṃ bhakṣayetkṣaudrai rgulmaplīhādikaṃ jayet 213
raso vidyādharo nāma gomūtraṃ ca pibedanu 214
paktiśūle trinerasaḥ
ṭaṅkaṇaṃ hāriṇaṃ śṛṅgaṃ svarṇaṃ śulbaṃ mṛtaṃ rasam
dinaikamārdra kadrā vairmardyaṃ ruddhvā puṭe pacet
trinetrākhyarasasyaikaṃ māṣaṃ madhvājyakairlihet 215
saindhavaṃ jīrakaṃ hiṃgu madhvājyābhyāṃ lihedanu
paktiśūlaharaḥ khyāto māsamātrānna saṃśayaḥ 216
śūle śūlagajakesarī rasaḥ
śuddhaṃ sūtaṃ dvidhā gandhaṃ yāmaikaṃ mardayed dṛḍham
dvayostulyaṃ śuddhatāmraṃ sampuṭe tannirodhayet 217
ūrdhvādho lavaṇaṃ dattvā mṛdbhāṇḍe dhārayedbhiṣak
tato gajapuṭe paktvā svāṅgaśītaṃ samuddharet 218
sampuṭaṃ cūrṇayetsūkṣmaṃ parṇakhaṇḍe dviguñjakam
bhakṣayetsarvaśūlārtto hiṅgu śuṇṭhī ca jīrakam 219
vacāmaricajaṃ cūrṇaṃ karṣamuṣṇajalaiḥ pibet
asādhyaṃ nāśayecchūlaṃ raso'ya gajakesarī 220
agnimāndyādau agnituṇḍīvaṭīrasaḥ
śuddhaṃ sūtaṃ viṣaṃ gandhamajamodāṃ phalatrayam
svarjikṣāraṃ yavakṣāraṃ vahnisaindhavajīrakam 221
sauvarcalaṃ viḍaṅgāni sāmudraṃ tr! yūṣaṇaṃ samam
viṣamuṣṭiṃ sarvatulyāṃ jambīrāmlena mardayet 222
maricābhāṃ vaṭīṃ khādedvahnimāndyapraśāntaye 223
viṣūcikāyām ajīrṇakaṇṭakarasaḥ
śuddhasūtaṃ viṣaṃ gandhaṃ samaṃ sarvaṃ vicūrṇayet
maricaṃ sarvatulyāṃśaṃ kaṇṭakāryāḥ phaladra vaiḥ 224
mardayedbhāvayetsarvamekaviṃśativārakam
vaṭīṃ guñjātrayaṃ khādetsarvājīrṇapraśāntaye
ajīrṇakaṇṭakaḥ so'ya raso hanti viṣūcikām 225
kapharoge manthānabhairavarasaḥ
mṛtaṃ sūtaṃ mṛtaṃ tāmraṃ hiṅgu puṣkaramūlakam
saindhavaṃ gandhakaṃ tālaṃ kaṭukīṃ cūrṇayetsamam 226
punarnavādevadālīnirguṇḍītaṇḍulīyakaiḥ

vātaroge vātanāśanarasaḥ
sūtahāṭakavajrāṇi tāmraṃ lohaṃ ca mākṣikam

pippalīmūlajakvāthaṃ sakṛṣṇamanupāyayet 231
sarvānvātavikārāṃstu nihantyākṣepakādikān 232
sannipātādau kanakasundaro rasaḥ
kanakasyāṣṭaśāṇāḥ syuḥ sūto dvādaśabhirmataḥ
gandho'pi dvādaśa proktastāmraṃśāṇadvayonmitam
abhrakaṃ syāccatuḥ śāṇaṃ mākṣikaṃ ca dviśāṇikam 233
vaṅgo dviśāṇaḥ saubīraṃ triśāṇaṃ lohamaṣṭakam
viṣaṃ triśāṇikaṃ kṛtvā lāṅgalī palasaṃmitā 234
mardayeddinamekaṃ ca rasairamlaphalodbhavaiḥ
dadyānmṛdupuṭaṃ vahnau tataḥ sūkṣmaṃ vicūrṇayet 235
māṣamātro raso deyaḥ sannipāte sudāruṇe
ārdra kasvarasenaiva rasonasya rasena vā 236
kilāsaṃ sarvakuṣṭhāni visarpaṃ ca bhagandaram
