jvare pathyāpathyam
raktaśālyādayaḥ śastāḥ purāṇāḥ ṣaṣṭikaiḥ saha
yavāgvodanalājārthe jvaritānāṃ jvarāpahāḥ
mudgān masūrāṃścaṇakān kulatthān samakuṣṭhakān
yūṣārthe yūṣasātmyānāṃ jvaritānāṃ pradāpayet
paṭolapatraṃ vārttākuṃ kulakaṃ kāravellakam
karkoṭakaṃ parpaṭakaṃ gojihvāṃ bālamūlakam
patraṃ guḍūcyāḥ śākārthaṃ jvaritānāṃ jvarāpaham
māṃsārthameṇalāvādīn yuktyā dadyādvicakṣaṇaḥ
kukkuṭāṃśca mayūrāṃśca tittirikrauñcavarttakān
gurūṣṇatvānna śaṃsanti jvare keciccikitsakāḥ
laṅghanenānilabalaṃ jvare yadyadhikaṃ bhavet
bhiṣaṅmātrāvikalpajño dadyāt tānapi kālavit
drā kṣādāḍimakharjūrapriyālaiḥ saparūṣakaiḥ
tarpaṇārhasya dātavyaṃ tarpaṇaṃ jvaranāśanam
kṛśo'lpadoṣo yaḥ kṣīṇakapho jīrṇajvarānvitaḥ
vibandhāsṛṣṭadoṣaśca rūkṣaḥ pittānilajvarī
pipāsārttaḥ sadāhaśca payasā sa sukhī bhavet
jīrṇajvare kaphe kṣīṇe kṣīraṃ syādamṛtopamam
tadeva taruṇe pītaṃ viṣavaddhanti mānavam
vyāyāmañca vyavāyañca snānaṃ caṅkramaṇantathā
jvaramukto na seveta yāvanno balavān bhavet
jantorjvaravimuktasya snānaṃ kuryāt punarjvaram
tasmājjvaravimukto'pi snānaṃ viṣamiva tyajet
balavarṇāgnivapuṣāṃ yāvanna prakṛtirbhavet
tāvajjvareṇa mukto'pi varjanīyāni varjayet
2 atīsāre pathyāpathyam
atīsāre'pi pathyādi jñeyaṃ sarvamajīrṇavat
snānāvagāhāvabhyaṅgaṃ gurusnindhadra vāśanam
vyāyāmamagnisantāpamatīsārī vivarjayet
3 grahaṇīroge pathyāpathyam
sujaraṃ dīpanaṃ vahnerannaṃ pānañca nityaśaḥ
seveta matimānatra viparītaṃ vivarjayet
śāli pratnaṃ masūrañca yavaṃ māṃsarasantathā
madgurañca tathā śṛṅgīṃ takraṃ bilvañca dāḍimam
śṛṅgāṭakaṃ chāgadugdhaṃ vārtākuñca kaśerukam
grahaṇīgadavān nityaṃ bhuñjītaivaṃvidhāni ca
divāsvapnaṃ surāṃ tīkṣṇāṃ rātrau jāgaraṇantathā
guru cānnamabhiṣyandi yatnataḥ parivarjayet
4 ajīrṇāgnimāndyayoḥ pathyāpathyam
durjaraṃ santyajet sarvaṃ niśāyāmaśanantathā
ajīrṇī mandavahniśca bhakṣayet sujaraṃ laghu
5 arśoroge pathyāpathyam
vātānulomanaṃ yad yat saraṃ vahnipradīpanam
sujaraṃ puṣṭidaṃ tat tadannapānaṃ hitaṃ matam
vegāvarodhaṃ strīpṛṣṭhayānamutkaṭamāsanam
yathāsvaṃ doṣalañcānnamarśasaḥ parivarjayet
6 krimiroge pathyāpathyam
pratyahaṃ kaṭukaṃ tiktaṃ bhojanaṃ kaphanāśanam
krimīṇāṃ nāśanaṃ rucyamagnisandīpanaṃ param
kṣīrāṇi māṃsāni ghṛtāni cāpi dadhīni śākāni ca parṇavanti
amlañca miṣṭañca rasaṃ viśeṣāt krimīñjighāṃsuḥ parivarjayeddhi
7 kāmalā halīmaka pāṇḍurogeṣu pathyāpathyam
purāṇayavagodhūmaśālayaśca punarnavā
mudgāḍhakī masūrāṇāṃ yūṣo jāṅgalajo rasaḥ
paṭolaṃ vṛddhakūṣmāṇḍaṃ taruṇaṃ kadalīphalam
matsyeṣu madguraḥ śṛṅgī takraṃ dhātr! yabhayā ghṛtam
rasonaḥ pakvamāmrañca vārttākuramṛtā niśā
ityādyāni gade pāṇḍau hitānyuktāni paṇḍitaiḥ
dhūmapānaṃ vegarodhaḥ svedanaṃ maithunaṃ surā
divāsvapno mṛdaśanaṃ rāmaṭhaṃ māṣasarṣapau
tīkṣṇāmlalavaṇādhyāśagurvannāni jalaṃ bahu
patraśākāni śimbī ca gade pāṇḍau na śarmaṇe
8 raktapitte pathyāpathyam
purāṇāḥ śāligodhūmayavā mudgamasūrakau
caṇakastuvarī vṛddhakūṣmāṇḍaṃ kadalīphalam
paṭolamapi vetrāgraṃ phalaṃ panasatālayoḥ
bilvadāḍimakharjūraṃ dhātrī drā kṣā udumbaram
parūṣaṃ nārikelañca kapitthañca kaśerukam
gavyaṃ māhiṣamājaṃ vā sarpiśchāgaṃ payastathā
śaśaiṇaharitacchāgā bakapārāvatādayaḥ
ekṣavaṃ śītasalilaṃ candanaṃ candra raśmayaḥ
mano'nukūlamākhyānaṃ śrutiramyañca kīrtanam
pīnonnatastanaśroṇiramyāṇāṃ sukhaveśmanām
rūpayauvanamattānāmāśleṣā ramaṇaṃ vinā
evaṃvidhāni sarvāṇi hitāni raktapittinām
tīkṣṇaṃ vidāhi viṣṭambhi pānānnaṃ kaupamambu ca
tāmbūlaṃ dadhi vārtākurmatsyo māṣaśca sarṣapaḥ
rasonakṣāraniṣpāvakulatthāśca guḍaḥ surā
hastyaśvayānaṃ vyāyāmaḥ krodhaḥ svapnaviparyayaḥ
vyāyāmo'dhvāṭanaṃ pāṭhaḥ santāpo vahnibhāsvatoḥ
raktasrāvo dhūmapānaṃ lobhaścapalatā tathā
evaṃvidhāni sarvāṇi varjanīyāni nityaśaḥ
nidānaṃ raktapittasya yatkiñcit samprakāśitam
jīvanārogyakāmaistanna sevyaṃ raktapittibhiḥ
9 kāsaroge pathyāpathyam
śāliṣaṣṭikagodhūma śyāmākayavakodra vāḥ
māṣamudgakulatthānāṃ rasaḥ sarpiḥ purātanam
vāstūkaṃ vāyasīśākaṃ vārttākurbālamūlakam
chāgaṃ dugdhaṃ ghṛtaṃ chāgaṃ sukhoṣṇasalilaṃ madhu
dhanvānūpabhavānāñca māṃsaṃ māṃsāśināntathā
drā kṣādāḍimakharjūraphalānyatra hitāni ca
vastirnasyamasṛṅmokṣo vyāyāmo dantagharṣaṇam
ātapo duṣṭapavano rajomārganiṣevaṇam
viṣṭambhīni vidāhīni rūkṣāṇi vividhāni ca
śakṛnmūtrodgārakāsavamivegavidhāraṇam
matsyaḥ kandaḥ sarṣapaśca duṣṭāmbhastumbupodikā
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ grāmyadharmmaḥ kāse'hitāni ca
10 yakṣmaṇi pathyāpathyam
godhūmaścaṇako mudgo raktaśāli purātanaḥ
chāgaṃ māṃsaṃ payaśchāṃgaṃ chāgaṃ sarpiśca śarkarā
matsyaṇḍikā ca madirā kastūrī sitacandanam
harmyaṃ srajaḥ smarakathā yuvatīnāñca darśanam
maṇimuktādibhūṣāṇāṃ dhāraṇaṃ pavano mṛduḥ
dhātr! yāmrapanasānāñca phalāni vakulaṃ phalam
gītaṃ hāsyaṃ candra raśmirgiraḥ śrutisukhapradāḥ
evaṃbhūtāni sarvāṇi śubhānyuktāni śoṣiṇām
rūkṣānnapānaṃ viṣamamaśanañca vidāhi yat
kaṭutiktakaṣāyāmlaśākamāṣarasonakāḥ
śimbī matsyaśca tāmbūlaṃ vyāyāmo vegadhāraṇam
sāhasāni ca karmāṇi śramaḥ svedanamañjanam
uccaiḥ sambhāṣaṇaṃ mārgasevanaṃ niśi jāgaraḥ
viśeṣato nidhuvanaṃ karmaurasyamathetarat
nidānatvena gaditaṃ yacca hetucatuṣṭayam
sarvāṇyetāni niyataṃ varjanīyāni yakṣmaṇi
yat pathyaṃ yadapathyaṃ ca raktapitte mayoditam
yakṣmaṇyapi ca tat pathyamapathyañcāpi tanmatam
śokaṃ striyaḥ krodhamasūyatāñca tyajedudārān viṣayān bhajecca
tathā dvijātīṃstridaśān gurūṃśca vācaśca puṇyāḥ śṛṇuyād dvijebhyaḥ
11 hikkāśvāse pathyāpathyam
śāliṣaṣṭikagodhūmayavāḥ śastāḥ purātanāḥ
śaśo'hibhuk tittiraśca dakṣo dhanyamṛgaḥ śukaḥ
sarpiḥ purātanaṃ chāgaṃ dugdhaṃ madhu surā tathā
jīvantikā ca vāstūkaṃ rasonaśca paṭolakam
drā kṣā truṭiḥ pauṣkarañca vāryuṣṇaṃ kaṭukatrayam
hikkāśvāseṣu jānīyādityādyāni hitāni hi
vidāhi gurupānānnaṃ vyāyāmādhvaniṣevaṇam
śvāsī hikkī grāmyadharmaṃ krodhaṃ cintāñca santyajet
12 arocake pathyam
rocanaṃ dīpanaṃ vahnerjihvākaṇṭhaviśodhanam
pathyaṃ śeṣamapathyam
13 chardiroge pathyāpathyam
purāṇāḥ śālayo lājā godhūmaśca yavo madhu
śaśalāvamayūrādyājāṅgalāḥ paśupakṣiṇaḥ
jambīrāmalakīdrā kṣādāḍimaṃ bījapūrakam
nārikelañca yadbālaṃ tattoyañca sitā surā
manojñagandhasaṃsevā candanādyanulepanam
śiraḥ snānaṃ sukhāsyā ca hitānicchardirogiṇām
yadugramudvegakaraṃ karma dra vyamathāpi vā
tyājyaṃ tadakhilaṃ chardyāṃ dhīmatārogyakāṅkṣiṇā
14 svarabhede pathyāpathyam
balapuṣṭipradaṃ hṛdyaṃ kaphaghnaṃ svaraśuddhikṛt
annaṃ pānañca nikhilaṃ svarabhede hitaṃ matam
nātrābhiṣyandi saṃsevyaṃ na ca śītakriyā hitā
divāsvāpo na karttavyo na ca vegavidhāraṇam
15 tṛṣṇāroge pathyāpathyam
hṛdyaṃ sumadhuraṃ śītaṃ seveta tṛṣayā'ditaḥ
ugramudvegajananaṃ tyajet sarvamatandri taḥ
16 mūrcchāroge pathyāpathyam
yavo lohitaśāliśca vārtākuścapaṭolakam
yūṣo jāṅgalamāṃsasya rohitādyāstathā jhaṣāḥ
dhāroṣṇaṃ gopayastakraṃ snānaṃ nadyā jale'male
hitānyetāni mūrcchāyāṃ saṃnyāsākhye tathā gade
tīkṣṇaṃ dra vyaṃ kriyā tīkṣṇā vegānāñca vidhāraṇam
krodhaśokādibhirbhāvairityetairvarddhate gadaḥ
17 madātyaye pathyāpathyam
hitā madātyaye pratnāḥ śālimudgayavāḥ sitā
payaḥ paṭolaṃ kharjūraṃ dāḍimaṃ nārikelakam
drā kṣādhātrī vicitrānnaṃ hṛdyaṃ madyaṃ parūṣakam
lāvatittiradakṣaiṇaśaśacchāgādijo rasaḥ
śiśiraḥ pavano dhārāgṛhaṃ candra sya raśmayaḥ
candanālepanaṃ snānaṃ priyāliṅganameva ca
tāmbūlaṃ dhūmapānañca lavaṇaṃ svedanāñjane
varjyānyakhilatīkṣṇāni vyādhau madyasamudbhave
18 dāhe pathyāpathyam
śālayaḥ ṣaṣṭikā mudgā masūrāścaṇakā yavāḥ
dhanvamāṃsarasā lājamaṇḍaśca lājaśaktavaḥ
śatadhautaṃ ghṛtaṃ dugdhaṃ navanītaṃ payobhavam
sitā kūṣmāṇḍakaṃ mocaṃ panasaṃ svādu dāḍimam
paṭolaṃ parpaṭaṃ drā kṣā dhātrīphalaparūṣake
śimbī tumbī payaḥ peṭī kharjūraṃ dhānyakaṃ misiḥ
bālatālaṃ priyālañca śṛṅgāṭakakaśeruke
madhūkapuṣpaṃ hrīberaṃ tiktāni nikhilāni ca
śītāḥ pradehā bhūveśma seko'bhyaṅgo'vagāhanam
padmotpaladalakṣauma śayyāśītalakānanam
kathā vicitrā gītāni kāminī parirambhaṇam
uśīracandanālepaśītāmbhaḥ śiśiro'nilaḥ
sudhāṃśuraśmayaḥ snānaṃ maṇayo madhurā rasāḥ
evaṃ cānyāni dāheṣu sevyāni sukhamīpsubhiḥ
viruddhānyannapānāni krodho vegavidhāraṇam
gajāśvayānamadhvā ca kṣāraṃ pittakarāṇi ca
vyāyāmaścātapastakraṃ tāmbūlaṃ madhu rāmaṭham
vyavāyaḥ kaṭutiktoṣṇānyahitānīti niścitam
19 unmāde pathyāpathyam
raktaśāliryavo mudgo godhūmaḥ kaurmamāmiṣam
dhanvodbhavaraso drā kṣā kapitthaṃ nārikelakam
vāstūkañca tathā brāhmī kūṣmāṇḍasya phalaṃ mahat
paṭolaṃ dhāroṣṇapayaḥ śatadhautaṃ tathā haviḥ
purātanaṃ nūtanaṃ ca suśītamanulepanam
hitānyuktānyathonmāde viruddhamaśanaṃ surā
uṣṇāśanaṃ tīkṣṇavīryaṃ patraśākaṃ kaṭhillakam
tiktāni nikhilānyeva vyavāyo niśi jāgaraḥ
nidrā tṛṣṇākṣudhādīnāṃ balād vegavidhāraṇam
sarvāṇi krūrakarmāṇi matāni na śubhāya ca
20 apasmāre pathyāpathyam
sarpiḥ purātanaṃ mudgo godhūmā raktaśālayaḥ
kūrmāmiṣaṃ dhanvaraso dugdhaṃ brāhmīdalaṃ vacā
paṭolaṃ vṛddhakuṣmāṇḍaṃ vāstūkaṃ svādu dāḍimam
śobhāñjanaṃ nārikelaṃ parūṣāmalake tathā
evaṃvidhāni cānyāni sukhadāni smṛtikṣaye
cintā śoko bhayaṃ krodhastvaśucīnyaśanāni ca
madyaṃ matsyo viruddhānnaṃ tīkṣṇoṣṇagurubhojanam
āyāso'tivyavāyaśca pūjyapūjāvyatikramaḥ
bimbyāṣāḍhaphalaṃ śākaṃ nidrā kṣuttṛḍvinigrahaḥ
toyāvagāhanaṃ śailadrumādyārohaṇantathā
ityādīni smṛtidhvaṃse varjanīyāni yatnataḥ
21 āmavāte pathyāpathyam
vāstūkaśākaṃ sāriṣṭaṃ śākaṃ paunarnavaṃ hitam
paṭolaṃ laśunañcaiva vārtākuṃ kāravellakam
yavānnaṃ koradūṣānnaṃ purāṇaṃ śāliṣaṣṭikam
lāvakānāṃ tathā māṃsaṃ hitaṃ takreṇa saṃskṛtam
sitaṃ ca yūṣaṃ kaulatthaṃ kālāyaṃ caṇakasya ca
rucyaṃ dadyādyathāsātmyamāmavātahitañca yat
dadhi matsyo guḍaḥ kṣīraṃ duṣṭanīramupodikā
viruddhamaśanaṃ pūrvo vāyuvegasya rodhanam
niśāyāṃ jāgaraḥ śītatoyasya pariṣevaṇam
na hitānyanile sāme vyavāyātiśayopi ca
22 śūlaroge pathyāpathyam
purāṇāḥ śālayaḥ kṣīramuṣṇaṃ jāṅgalajo rasaḥ
paṭolaṃ kāravellañca drā kṣā pakvāmradāḍimau
viḍaṃ śāliñca patrāṇi taptāmbho devapuṣpakam
anulomakarāṇyatra sarvāṇyeva hitāni vai
vyāyāmaṃ maithunaṃ madyaṃ lavaṇaṃ kaṭuvaidalam
vegarodhaṃ śucaṃ krodhaṃ varjayecchūlavānnaraḥ
23 udāvarttānāhayoḥ pathyāpathyam
sujarañca saraṃ yad yadannaṃ pānañca puṣṭidam
udāvarte tathānāhe sevyaṃ varjyaṃ yato'nyathā
24 hṛdro ge pathyāpathyam
purātano raktaśālirjāṅgalā mṛgapakṣiṇaḥ
kulatthamudgayūṣāśca paṭolaṃ kadalīphalam
rasālaṃ vṛddhakūṣmāṇḍaṃ dāḍimañca harītakī
drā kṣā takraṃ saindhavañca hitāni hṛdayāmaye
vegarodho vyavāyaśca vyāyāmo niśi jāgaraḥ
sahyavindhyasamudbhūtasaritāṃ salilantathā
meṣīpayo jalaṃ duṣṭaṃ gurutiktāmlabhojanam
patraśākañcādhyaśanaṃ na hitāni hṛdāmaye
25 udararoge pathyāpathyam
abdotpannā raktaśāliryavamudgakulatthakāḥ
mākṣikañca surā sīdhurjāṅgalā mṛgapakṣiṇaḥ
rasonamārdra kaṃ takraṃ kulakaṃ śigrujaṃ phalam
punarnavā kāravellaṃ tāmbūlaile payastathā
laghvannaṃ dīpanaṃ tiktaṃ vīkṣya doṣānalau balam
yuñjyādudariṇe vaidya ityādyāni yathātatham
payo'tipānaṃ gurvannaṃ snehanaṃ dhūmasevanam
audakānūpamāṃsāni patraśākaṃ tilo dadhi
lavaṇāśanamuṣṇāni vidāhīnyambu doṣavat
mahendrā dri bhavānāñca saritāṃ salilantathā
vyāyāmaśca vyavāyaśca snānaṃ caṅkramaṇantathā
evaṃvidhāni cānyāni tyājyānyudaribhiḥ sadā
26 gulme pathyāpathyam
vallūraṃ mūlakaṃ matsyān śuṣkaśākāni vaidalam
na khādeccālukaṃ gulmī madhurāṇi phalāni ca
27 mūtrāghāte pathyāpathyam
mūtrakṛcchre'śmarīroge yatpathyaṃ ca prayujyate
mūtrāghāteṣu sarveṣu tadyuñjyād deśakālavit
28 mūtrakṛcchre pathyāpathyam
annapānamanugraṃ yanmūtralañcānulomanam
hitamatra vijānīyādviparītaṃ sukhāya na
29 aśmaryām pathyāpathyam
vraṇakriyāṃ hate śalye pathyenaināñca varttayet
30 pramehe pathyāpathyam
śyāmākakodra voddālagodhūmacaṇakāḍhakī
kulatthāśca hitā bhojye purāṇā mehināṃ sadā
jāṅgalaṃ tiktaśākañca yavānnañca śramo madhu
etadanyaccharkarādyaṃśleṣmalañca na śarmaṇe
31 somaroge pathyāpathyam
yavagodhūmamāṃsāni kṣīramuddhṛtasārakam
vyāyāmo bhramaṇañcāpi hitāya somarogiṇām
ekṣavañcāmbupānañca phalamāmasukhāsanam
ahitāya vinirdiṣṭaṃ bhiṣagbhiḥ śāstrakovidaiḥ
32 sthaulye pathyāpathyam
purāṇaśālayo mudgakulatthoddālakodra vāḥ
lekhanā bastayaścaiva sevyā medasvinā sadā
śramacintāvyavāyādhvakṣaudra jāgaraṇapriyaḥ
hantyavaśyamatisthaulyaṃ yavaśyāmākabhojanaiḥ
asvapnañca vyavāyañca vyāyāmaṃ cintanāni ca
sthaulyamicchan parityaktuṃ krameṇātipravarddhayet
prātarmadhuyutaṃ vāri sevitaṃ sthaulyanāśanam
uṣṇamannasya maṇḍaṃ vā piban kṛśatanurbhavet
snānaṃ rasāyanaṃ śālīn godhūmān sukhaśīlatām
kṣīrekṣu vikṛtīrmāṣān sauhityaṃ snehanāni ca
svabhāvasthatvamanvicchan medasvī parivarjayet
33 śotharoge pathyāpathyam
purāṇāḥ śālayo mudgā yavāḥ śimbī punarnavā
raktaśigrurasonau ca māṇamūlaṃ paṭolakam
tāmracūḍamayūrādikhagānāmāmiṣairasam
śṛṅgīmadgurayoryūṣo yūṣaḥ kūrmāmiṣodbhavaḥ
nimbapatraṃ haridrā ca tiktāni dīpanāni ca
ityādyāni vijānīyāddhitāni śvayathau bhiṣak
gurvannaṃmadyamamlañca śītatoyaṃ vidāhi ca
divāsvāpaṃ maithunañca śotharogīḥ parityajet
34 vṛddhiroge pathyāpathyam
anabhiṣyandi pānānnaṃ nātiśītā kriyā tathā
vṛddhiroge hitāya syādviparītaṃ vivarjjayet
35 galagaṇḍādiroge pathyāpathyam
yavamudgapaṭolāni kaṭurūkṣañca bhojanam
chardiṃ śoṇitamokṣañca galagaṇḍe prayojayet
vātakaphavṛddhikarā yogā apathyāḥ
36 arbudaroge pathyāpathyam
purāṇaghṛtapānañca jīrṇalohitaśālayaḥ
yavā mudgāḥ paṭolañca raktaśigruḥ kaṭhillakam
śāliñcaśākaṃ vetrāgraṃ rūkṣāṇi ca kaṭūni ca
dīpanāni ca sarvāṇi gugguluśca śilājatu
galagaṇḍe gaṇḍamālā'pacīgranthyarbudāntare
yathādoṣaṃ yathā'vasthaṃ pathyametat prakīrttitam
dugdhekṣuvikṛtiḥ sarvā māṃsaṃ cānūpasambhavam
piṣṭānnamamlaṃ madhuraṃ gurvabhiṣyandakāri ca
arbude'pathyametaddhi varjyamarbudarogibhiḥ
37 ślīpadaroge pathyāpathyam
kaphaghnaṃ sārakaṃ pānamannaṃ vahnikarañca yat
nābhiṣyandakaraṃ pathyaṃ heyamanyadvijānatā
38 vidra dhivraṇaroge pathyāpathyam
jīrṇaśālyodanaṃ snigdhaṃ jīvantī ca punarnavā
paṭolaṃ mudgayūṣaśca hitānyetāni santatam
amlaṃ dadhi ca śākaṃ ca māṃsamānūpamaudakam
kṣīraṃ gurūṇi cānnāni vraṇe ca parivarjayet
39 vraṇaśothe pathyāpathyam
śothe yānyannapānāni bheṣajāni hitāni ca
tāni sarvāṇi jānīyād vraṇe syuḥ śarmaṇe sadā
āgantujavraṇe pathyāpathyam
śārīravraṇavaccātra kriyā kāryā bhiṣagvaraiḥ
41 bhagnādhikāre pathyāpathyam
māṃsaṃ māṃsarasaḥ kṣīraṃ sapiryūṣaḥ kalāyajaḥ
bṛṃhaṇañcānnapānañca deyaṃ bhagnāya jānatā
lavaṇaṃ kaṭukaṃ kṣāramamlaṃ maithunamātapam
vyāyāmañca na seveta bhagno rūkṣānnameva ca
42 nāḍīvraṇe pathyāpathyam
yadvāyorānulomyāya yaccāgnibalavṛddhaye
yaccāsraśuddhyai tatpathyamato'nyaccātra garhitam
43 bhagandare pathyāpathyam
sarṣapaḥ śālimudgau ca vilepī jāṅgalo rasaḥ
paṭolaṃ śigru vetrāgraṃ pattūraṃ bālamūlakam
tilasarṣapayostailaṃ tiktavargo ghṛtaṃ madhu
evaṃvidhāni cānyāni bhagandarahitāni hi
viruddhānyannapānāni viṣamāśanamātapam
vyāyāmaṃ maithunaṃ yuddhaṃ pṛṣṭhayānaṃ gurūṇi ca
saṃvatsaraṃ parihareduparūḍhavraṇo naraḥ
44 upadaṃśe pathyāpathyam
raktaśāli yavaṃ mudgaṃ ghṛtaṃ śigruphalantathā
paṭolaṃ tiktavargañca niṣevetopadaṃśavān
divānidrā ñca gurvannaṃ vegasandhāraṇaṃ guḍam
madyamāyāsamamlañca varjayedupadaṃśavān
pāpapramehī vātāsrī kuṣṭhī pāpopadaṃśavān
na bhajedaṅganāṃ nāpi tadgadinyaṅganā naram
45 śūkadoṣe pathyāpathyam
śūkarogeṣu pathyāni sarpiḥ śāliryavo vacā
mudgayūṣo dāḍimañca paṭolaṃ bālamūlakam
śigruṃ karkoṭakaṃ caiva vetrāgraṃ ca kaṭhillakam
pattūraṃ saindhavaṃ tailaṃ kūpasya salilantathā
dhāraṇaṃ mūtravegasya divānidrā ca maithunaṃ
vyāyāmo gurubhojyañca na hitāni tathā guḍāḥ
46 kuṣṭheṣu pathyāpathyam
purāṇāḥ śālayo mudgā āḍhakyaśca masūrakāḥ
yavā nimbasya patrāṇi paṭolaṃ bṛhatīphalam
cakramardadalaṃ meṣaśṛṅgañca hilamocikā
koṣātakī ca vetrāgraṃ pakvaṃ tālaṃ punarnavā
gokharoṣṭrāśvamahiṣīmūtraṃ sarpirvi recanam
nikhilāni ca tiktāni kuṣṭharoge hitāni ca
pāpaṃ karma divānidrā viruddhaviṣamāśanam
vyāyāmo vegarodhaśca sūryyaraśmiśca maithunam
gurudra vanavānnānāṃ bhojanañca guḍo dadhi
dugdhaṃ madyamāmiṣaṃ ca matsyo māṣastilastathā
ikṣuramlaṃ mūlakañca viṣṭambhi ca vidāhakṛt
evaṃvidhāni cānyāni kuṣṭhe varjyāni nityaśaḥ
47 kṣudra roge pathyāpathyam
vātānulomanaṃ yacca śakṛnmūtrapravarttanam
śodhanaṃ śoṇitasyāpi tridoṣaghnāni yāni ca
dra vyāṇi kṣudra rogeṣu hitānyevaṃvidhāni ca
viparītāni sarvāṇi varjanīyāni yatnataḥ
48 visphoṭe pathyāpathyam
raktadoṣaharaṃ yad yad yad yatpittapraṇāśanam
sarvamatra prayoktavyaṃ vivicya bhiṣajā sadā
49 masūrikāroge pathyāpathyam
yathātathaṃ pratīkāryyā jvarakāsādayaśca te
uccaistame praśaste ca masūrīrogapīḍitaḥ
śuṣke gṛhe vasennityaṃ gurūṣṇavasanāvṛtaḥ
śītaṃ vātaṃ jalaṃ śītaṃ vahnitāpaṃ tathātapam
tyajet kāntāṃ divāsvāpaṃ yātrāṃ rātrau ca jāgaram
kiñciduṣṇāmbunā svedaḥ romāntījvaranāśanaḥ
pūrvaṃ laṅghanavāntirecanaśirāvedhāḥ śaśāṅkojjvalā
jīrṇā ṣaṣṭikaśālayo'pi caṇakā mudgā masūrā yavāḥ
sarve'pi pratudāḥ kapotacaṭakā dātyūhakrauñcādayo
jīvañjīvaśukādayo'pi kulakaṃ kāṭhillamāṣāḍhakam
itthaṃ sarvadaśāvibhāgavihitaṃ pathyaṃ yathādoṣataḥ
saṃyuktaṃ mudamātanoti nitarāṃ nṝṇāṃ masūrīgade
rataṃ khedaṃ śramaṃ tailaṃ gurvannaṃ krodhamātapam
duṣṭāmbu duṣṭapavanaṃ viruddhānyaśanāni ca
niṣpāvamālukaṃ śākaṃ lavaṇaṃ viṣamāsanam
kaṭvamlaṃ vegarodhañca masūrīgadavāṃstyajet
50 visarpe pathyāpathyam
tiktavargo'khilaścaiva pānānnamavidāhakam
dra vyaṃ śoṇitasaṃśuddhikaraṃ candanalepanam
anudvegakaraṃ karma visarpe paramaṃ hitam
viparītaṃ vijānīyāt kleśadaṃ gadavṛddhikṛt
51 śītapittādiṣu pathyāpathyam
purāṇāḥ śālayaḥ śastā yūṣo mudgakulatthayoḥ
karkoṭakaṃ kāravellaṃ śigrudrā rkṣā! ca dāḍimam
kaṭutailaṃ taptanīraṃ nikhilaṃ kaphapittahṛt
jñeyāni śītapittādigadeṣu śubhadāni hi
gurvannapānaṃ kṣīreṣu vikārān madhuraṃ rasam
amlañcāpyaudakānūpajīvānāmāmiṣaṃ tathā
snehaṃ madyaṃ navīnañca matsyaṃ prāgdakṣiṇānilam
pītamambu divāsvāpaṃ śītapittādimāṃstyajet
52 udardakoṭhādiroge pathyāpathyam
śālimudgakulatthāṃśca kāravellamupodikām
vetrāgraṃ taptanīrañca śleṣmapittaharāṇi ca
kaṭutiktakaṣāyāṇi sarvāṇīti gaṇaḥ sakhā
śītapittodardakoṭharogiṇāṃ syādyathāmatam
kṣīrekṣujātā vividhā vikārā matsyaudakānūpabhavāmiṣāṇi
navīnamadyaṃ vamivegarodhaḥ prāgdakṣiṇāśāpavano'hni nidrā
snānaṃ viruddhāśanamātapañca snigdhaṃ tathā'mla madhuraṃ vyavāyaḥ
gurvannapānāni ca śītapittakoṭhāmayodardavatāṃ viṣāṇi
53 amlapitte pathyāpathyam
yavagodhūmamudgāśca purāṇā raktaśālayaḥ
jalāni taptaśītāni śarkarā madhuśaktavaḥ
karkoṭakaṃ kāravellaṃ paṭolaṃ hilamocikā
vetrāgraṃ vṛddhakūṣmāṇḍaṃ rambhāpuṣpañca vāstukam
kapitthaṃ dāḍimaṃ dhātrī tiktāni sakalāni ca
amlapittāmaye nityaṃ sevitavyāni mānavaiḥ
navānnāni samastāni kaphapittakarāṇi ca
vamivegaṃ tilān māṣān kulatthāstailabhakṣaṇam
avidugdhañca dhānyāmlaṃ lavaṇāmlakaṭūni ca
gurvannaṃ dadhi madyañca varjayedamlapittavān
54 vātarakte pathyāpathyam
yavaṣaṣṭikanīvārakalāyāruṇaśālayaḥ
godhūmaścaṇako mudgastuvarī ca mukuṣṭhakaḥ
avyajāmahiṣīṇāñca gavāṃ sarpiḥ payastathā
upodikā kākamācī vetrāgraṃ suniṣaṇṇakam
vāstūkaṃ kāravellañca kūṣmāṇḍañca purātanam
paṭolaṃ tiktasaṅghaśca pathyāni vātaśoṇite
māṣaṃ kulatthaṃ niṣpāvaṃ kalāyaṃ mūlakaṃ dadhi
madyaṃ māṃsañca matsyañca kāñjikaṃ kṣārasevanam
divāsvapnaṃ vahnitāpaṃ vyāyāmaṃ maithunantathā
kaṭūṣṇagurvabhiṣyandi lavaṇāmlāni varjayet
55 vātarogeṣu pathyāpathyam
saṃvatsaroṣitāḥ śāliṣaṣṭikāstailasarpiṣī
grāmyānūpaudakānāñca yūṣo māṣakulatthayoḥ
navīnatilagodhūmavārttākulaśunāni ca
rohito madguraḥ śṛṅgī varmī ca kavayīlliśau
drā kṣādāḍimajambīraparūṣakaphalāni ca
snigdhoṣṇāni ca bhojyāni snigdhoṣṇamanulepanam
evaṃvidhāni sarvāṇi hitāni vātarogiṇām
tṛṇadhānyakalāyañca caṇako rājamāṣakaḥ
kaṭhillakañca niṣpāvabījaṃ bimbīkaśerukam
śītamambu viruddhānnaṃ vyavāyo bhramaṇaṃ bahu
evaṃ vidhāni sarvāṇi na hitānyanilāmaye
viśeṣādarditādhmānavatāṃ snānaṃ vigarhitam
56 ūrustambhe pathyāpathyam
bhojyāḥ purāṇā godhūmakodra voddālaśālayaḥ
jāṅgalairaghṛtairmāṃsaiḥ śākaiścālavaṇairhitaḥ
śākairalavaṇairdadyājjalatailājyasādhitaiḥ
suniṣaṇṇakanimbādyairjīrṇaṃ śālyodanaṃ bhiṣak
pratārayet pratisroto nadīṃ śītajalāṃ śivām
saraśca vimalaṃ śītaṃ sthiratoyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ
guruśītadra vasnigdhaviruddhāsātmyabhojanam
tyajedamlañca lavaṇamūrustambhagadārditaḥ
57 vepathuvāte pathyā pathyam
vātavyādhiṣu yat pathyaṃ yadapathyañca kīrttitam
jñeyaṃ vepathuvāte tat pathyañcāpathyameva ca
58 acala vāte pathyā pathyam
apasmāre ca mūrcchāyāṃ tathā vātāmaye'pi ca
yatpathyaṃ yadapathyañca tattadevātra sammatam
59 skhālitye pathyāpathyam
pathyamatra vijānīyād dra vyaṃ puṣṭibalapradam
60 khajjanikādhikāre pathyāpathyam
ārogyamicchatā tyājyaṃ khañjanī dvidalāśanam
nidānasevino yasmānna vyādhirvinivarttate
vātaghnaṃ poṣaṇaṃ yacca pānamannañca bheṣajam
prayojyamiha tatsarvaṃ vivicya bhiṣajā sadā
61 mukharoge pathyāpathyam
annapānādikaṃ yacca sujaraṃ vahnidīpanam
vraṇadoṣaharaṃ tattanmukharoge hitaṃ matam
dantakāṣṭhaṃ snānamamlaṃ matsyamānūpamāmiṣam
dadhi kṣīraṃ guḍaṃ māṣaṃ rūkṣānnaṃ kaṭhināśanam
adhomukhena śayanaṃ gurvabhiṣyandakāri ca
mukharogeṣu sarveṣu divā nidrā ñca varjayet
62 dantaroge'pathyam
phalānyamlāni śītāmbu rūkṣānnaṃ dantadhāvanam
tathā'tikaṭhinaṃ bhakṣyaṃ dantarogī vivarjayet
63 galaroge pathyam
ekakālaṃ yavānnañca bhuñjīta snigdhamalpaśaḥ
64 karṇaroge pathyāpathyam
svedo vireko vamanaṃ nasyaṃ dhūmaḥ śirāvyadhaḥ
godhūmāḥ śālayo mudgā yavāśca pratanaṃ haviḥ
lāvomayūro hariṇastittiro vanakukkuṭaḥ
paṭolaṃ śigru vārtākuḥ suniṣaṇṇaṃ kaṭhillakam
rasāyanāni sarvāṇi brahmacaryamabhāṣaṇam
upayuktaṃ yathādoṣamidaṃ karṇāmaye hitam
dantakāṣṭhaṃ śiraḥ snānaṃ vyāyāmaṃ śleṣmalaṃ guru
kaṇḍūyanaṃ tuṣārañcakarṇarogī parityajet
65 nāsāroge pathyāpathyam
snehaḥ svedaḥ śiro'bhyaṅgaḥ purāṇā yavaśālayaḥ
kulatthamudgayoryūṣo grāmyā jāṅgalajā rasāḥ
vārttākuḥ kulakaṃ śigruḥ karkoṭaṃ bālamūlakam
laśunaṃ dadhi taptāmbu vāruṇī kaṭukatrayam
kaṭvamlalavaṇaṃ snigdhamuṣṇaṃ ca laghu bhojanam
nāsāroge pīnasādau sevyametadyathābalam
snānaṃ krodhaṃ śakṛnmūtravātavegāñ śucaṃ dra vam
bhūmiśayyāṃ ca yatnena nāsārogī parityajet
66 śiroroge pathyāpathyam
śāli yavaṃ māṃsarasaṃ vārttākuñca paṭolakam
drā kṣādāḍimakharjūraphalāni ca payastathā
niśāpānaṃ nadīsnānaṃ gandhadra vyaniṣevaṇam
śirorogeṣu sarveṣu hitamuktaṃ yathāyatham
dra vyāṇi cātitīkṣṇāni durjarāṇi ca yāni vā
tānyaniṣṭapradānyatra tīkṣṇāśca nikhilāḥ kriyāḥ
67 netraroge pathyāpathyam
śālirmudgo yavo dugdhaṃ paṭolaṃ cāpyudumbaram
drā kṣādāḍimakharjūrāmalakānyavidāhi ca
puṣṭidāni supācyāni hitāni nayanāmaye
viparītāni jānīyād varjanīyāni yatnataḥ
68 dhvajabhaṅge pathyam
śāliṣaṣṭikagodhūmamasūracaṇakādayaḥ
haiyaṅgavīnadugdhe ca navanītaṃ surā sidhu
caṭako varttakaścaiva tittiraścaraṇāyudhaḥ
śaśahariṇacchāgānāṃ māṃsāni komalāni ca
drā kṣākharjūradāḍimajambvāmraphalāni ca
pathyānyetāni vastūni dhvajabhaṅgapraśāntaye
69 vājīkaraṇe
sarvaṃ vṛṣyam pathyam
yathā
yat kiñcinmadhuraṃ snigdhaṃ jīvanaṃ bṛṃhaṇaṃ guru
harṣaṇaṃ manasaścaiva sarvaṃ tad vṛṣyamucyate
apathyam
atyantamuṣṇakaṭutiktakapāyamamlaṃ kṣārañca śākamathavā lavaṇādhikañca
kāmī sadaiva ratimān vanitā'bhilāṣī no bhakṣayediti samastajanaprasiddhiḥ
70 śukramehe pathyāpathyam
abhiṣyandyatitīkṣṇañca pānānnaṃ vahnisūryayoḥ
santāpaṃ strīprasaktiñca vegarodhaṃ prajāgaram
krodhaṃ śokaṃ divānidrā ṃ! laṅghanañcāticintanam
atyālasyamasatsaṅgaṃ śukramehe vivarjayet
anugraṃ poṣaṇaṃ śukravarddhakaṃ yad bhavediha
annapānaṃ śubhaṃ jñeyaṃ viparītaṃ na śarmaṇe
71 pradare pathyāpathyam
kṣataghnaṃ poṣaṇaṃ yacca tadannamiha śarmaṇe
viparītaṃ vijānīyād varjjanīyaṃ viśeṣataḥ
72 yoniroge pathyāpathyam
bṛṃhaṇaṃ poṣaṇaṃ bahnerdīpanañcānulomanam
annaṃ pānaṃ yoniroge sevetānyadvivarjayet
73 garbhiṇyāḥ pathyāpathyam
śālayaḥ ṣaṣṭikā mudgā godhūmā lājaśaktavaḥ
navanītaṃ ghṛtaṃ kṣīraṃ rasālā madhu śarkarā
panasaṃ kadalī dhātrī drā kṣā'mla svādu śītalam
kastūrī candanaṃ mālā karpūramanulepanam
candri kā snānamabhyaṅgo mṛduśayyā himānilaḥ
santarpaṇaṃ priyāśleṣo vihāraśca manoramaḥ
priyaṅkaraṃ cānnapānaṃ garbhiṇīnāṃ hitaṃ sadā
svedanaṃ vamanaṃ kṣāraṃ kadannaṃ viṣamāśanam
apathyamidamuddiṣṭaṃ garbhiṇīnāṃ maharṣibhiḥ
74 stanaroge pathyāpathyam
taddoṣaśamanaṃ pānamannamauṣadhamācaret
bṛṃhaṇīyaṃ vidhiṃ kuryād dṛṣṭvā'dugdhau stanau striyaḥ
75 sūtikāroge pathyāpathyam
vyāyāmaṃ maithunaṃ krodhaṃ śītasevāñca varjayet
prasūtā yuktamāhāraṃ vihārañca samācaret
76 bālaroge pathyāpathyam
yatpathyaṃ yadapathyaṃ ca nṛṇāmuktaṃ jvarādiṣu
tattadvidheyamaucityād bālānāṃ teṣu jānatā
pūrvaṃ pathyamapathyañca mandāgnau yatprakīrttitam
aucityād yojayejjāte bālānāṃ pārigarbhike
āgantūnmādavātānāṃ pathyāpathyaṃ yadīritam
aucityād yojayettatra bāleṣu graharogiṣu
77 viṣaroge pathyāpathyam
śālayaḥ ṣaṣṭikāścaiva koradūṣāḥ priyaṅgavaḥ
mudgā hareṇavastailaṃ sarpiścāpi navaṃ kvacit
vārttākuḥ kulakaṃ dhātrī jīvantī taṇḍulīyakam
bhojanārthe viṣārttānāṃ hitaṃ paṭuṣu saindhavam
viruddhādhyaśanakrodhakṣudbhayāyāsamaithunam
varjayedviṣamukto'pi divāsvapnaṃ viśeṣataḥ
atha prathamapariśiṣṭoktarogāṇāṃ pathyāpathyavivecanā
plīhayakṛdro gayoḥ pathyāpathyam
jīrṇaṃjvare hitaṃ yad yad yad yattatrāhitaṃ matam
yakṛt plīhāmaye cāpi tathā jñeyaṃ hitāhitam
viśeṣo yakṛti
madyamagnyātapaṃ śrāntiṃ gurvannaṃ viṣamāśanam
tīkṣṇāśanaṃ divāsvāpaṃ niśi cāpi prajāgaram
tauryyatrikamathādhmānaṃ śokacintābhayāni ca
krodhāvegaṃ vegarodhaṃ yakṛdro gī parityajet
urastoyaroge pathyāpathyam
tato vyavāyamadhvānaṃ vyāyāmaṃ śiśiraṃ jalam
ahaḥsvāpaṃ śucaṃ krodhaṃ tyajed varṣaṃ gadotthitaḥ
gadodvege pathyāpathyam
hṛdyaṃ snigdhañca pānānnaṃ supācyaṃ dehapoṣaṇam
apadārthagade proktaṃ śubhāyānyatra śarmaṇe
śiai!śavai! saṃnyāse pathyāpathyam
yavo lohitaśāliśca vārtākuśca paṭolakam
yūṣo jāṅgalamāṃsasya rohitādyāstathā jhaṣāḥ
dhāroṣṇaṃ gopayastakraṃ snānaṃ nadyā jale'male
hitānyetāni mūrcchāyāṃ saṃnyāsākhye tathā gade
tīkṣṇaṃ dra vyaṃ kriyā tīkṣṇā vegānāñca vidhāraṇam
krodhaśokādyabhibhava ityetairvarddhate gadaḥ
yoṣā'pasmāre pathyāpathyam
yaddhātupoṣakaṃ pānamannamauṣadhameva ca
koṣṭhaśuddhikarañcāpi tattadatra prayojayet
tattvonmāde pathyāpathyam
dhāroṣṇaṃ gopayaḥ śastaṃ śālayaśca purātanāḥ
yavamudgatilāścāpi nikhilaṃ cānulomanam
parihāsaḥ priyaiḥ sārddhaṃ priyābhiśca sahāsanam
ityetāni hitānyatra viparītānyaśarmaṇe
snāyuśūlādhikāre pathyāpathyam
yat pathyaṃ yadapathyañca vātavyādhau prakīrttitam
tathaiva snāyuśūleṣu nirṇītaṃ vibudhairiti
tāṇḍavaroge pathyāpathyam
bṛṃhaṇaṃ pānamannañca snānaṃ srotasvatījale
śayanaṃ kleśaśūnyaṃ yat karma tacceha śarmaṇe
karṣaṇādyakhilaṃ proktamaśubhāya purātanaiḥ
klomaroge pathyāpathyam
anugrāṇyannapānāni klomāmayanipīḍitaḥ
sevetogrāṇi sarvāṇi yatnataḥ parivarjayet
vṛkke pathyāpathyam
pathyairbalyaiḥ supācyaiśca bhiṣagenaṃ prayāpayet
aupasargikamehe pathyāpathyam
bheṣajaṃ pānamannañca niṣevetānulomanam
vraṇaghnaṃ mūtrajananaṃ kriyāmugrāṃ vivarjayet
smaronmāde pathyāpathyam
hitaṃ prakīrtitañcātra śukramehaghnamauṣadham
vātānulomanaṃ yacca supācyaṃ vahnidīpanam
atrānnaṃ yojayet prājño viparītaṃ vivarjayet
śīrṣāmburoge pathyāpathyam
laghupuṣṭikaraṃ sarvaṃ pānamannaṃ sarañca yat
mastiṣkāmbuni tat pathyaṃ viparītaṃ hitāya na
mastiṣkavepane pathyāpathyam
payomāṃsarasādyañca snāyūnāṃ balavarddhanam
annapānādikaṃ yacca sujaraṃ svādu sārakam
śīrṣavepathurogibhyo hitaṃ tannikhilaṃ matam
viparītaṃ vijānīyāt kadācana na śarmadam
mastiṣkacayāpacaye pathyāpathyam
mastiṣkasya caye hrāse dehasya poṣaṇaṃ laghu
pānamannaṃ sukhāya syādviparītaṃ na śarmaṇe
aṃśughāte pathyāpathyam
annapānādikaṃ snigdhaṃ balapuṣṭipradaṃ saram
hitaṃ syādaṃśughātibhyo viparītaṃ na śarmaṇe
bādhake yio!nirogei! pathyāpathyam
bṛṃhaṇaṃ poṣaṇaṃ vahnerdīpanañcānulomanam
annapānaṃ yoniroge sevetānyadvivarjayet
yonikaṇḍvadhikāre pathyāpathyam
yad yad vahnikaraṃ pācyaṃ tathā vātānulomanam
annaṃ pānañcātra yojyaṃ viparītaṃ sukhāya na
yonyākṣepādhikāre pathyāpathyam
atra pathyaṃ ghṛtaṃ dugdhaṃ godhūmacaṇakādayaḥ
yūṣaśchāgādisambhūta ugravīryaṃ hitaṃ na hi
jarāyuroge pathyāpathyam
jarāyurogiṇī nārī na ca seveta pūruṣam
na khādedugravīryāṇi nāpi kuryyādatiśramam
aṇḍādhāre pathyāpathyam
pathyamatra havirdugdhaṃ śāli pratno yavastilaḥ
chāgamāṃsarasaścaiva dra vyamugraṃ na śarmmaṇe
ojomehe pathyāpathyam
laghu balyaṃ purāṇañca dhānyaṃ mudgayavādikam
vārttākuñca paṭolañca kākodumbarakaṃ tathā
kāravellādikaṃ śastaṃ varjjayenmadhuraṃ guru
māṃsaṃ matsyāṃstathā'dhvānamātapāgniniṣevaṇam
dūṣitātiśītatoyasnānapānāvagāhanam
lasikāmehe pathyāpathyam
raktaśālyodanaṃ mudgaṃ yavo vāstūkameva ca
pālakyā caiva vetrāgraṃ karkoṭī kadalī tathā
himālayapradeśe ca vāso vā susthacittatā
hitānetān niṣeveta gurvabhiṣyandi bhojanam
matsyaṃ māṃsaṃ tathā raudra sevā'dhvānaṃ pariśramam
varjjayed yatnato dhīmānāyurārogyavṛddhaye
pāradavikāre pathyāpathyam
vātarakte tathā kuṣṭhe pathyāni yāni tāni ca
śivatejobhave roge nirdiśet kuśalo bhiṣak
iti pathyāpathyavivecanātmakaṃ dvitīyaṃ pariśiṣṭaṃ samāptam
samāptaścā'yaṃ granthaḥ
